#### LEVEL 2: ADVERSARIAL STATE CONTAMINATION & SQLITE HANDLE TEARDOWN PROBE ($T_0 = \$0.00$)

Execute the colocated Phase 2 Vitest suite and run an inline adversarial state-contamination and SQLite handle-closure probe in an isolated temporary directory:

```bash
# Check 2.1: Execute Colocated Phase 2 Vitest Suite
npx vitest run packages/299.lore/07.database.unit/test/unitMemoryReset.test.ts

# Check 2.2: Adversarial State Contamination & SQLite Handle Close Probe
npx tsx -e "
import * as fs from 'node:fs';
import * as os from 'node:os';
import * as path from 'node:path';
import { TargetModel } from './packages/299.lore/34.target.unit/target.model.js';
import { resetTarget } from './packages/299.lore/34.target.unit/buz/target.buzz.js';
import { CanonModel } from './packages/299.lore/41.canon.unit/canon.model.js';
import { resetCanon } from './packages/299.lore/41.canon.unit/buz/canon.buzz.js';
import { CollectModel } from './packages/299.lore/97.collect.unit/collect.model.js';
import { resetCollect } from './packages/299.lore/97.collect.unit/buz/collect.buzz.js';
import { DatabaseModel } from './packages/299.lore/07.database.unit/database.model.js';
import { resetDatabase } from './packages/299.lore/07.database.unit/buz/database.buzz.js';

const tmpDir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'lore-phase2-audit-'));
const sqliteDir = path.join(tmpDir, 'data', 'sqlite');
fs.mkdirSync(sqliteDir, { recursive: true });
fs.mkdirSync(path.join(tmpDir, 'packages', '299.lore'), { recursive: true });
const dbFile = path.join(sqliteDir, 'gotham-trinity-crisis.local.db');
const walFile = path.join(sqliteDir, 'gotham-trinity-crisis.local.db-wal');
const shmFile = path.join(sqliteDir, 'gotham-trinity-crisis.local.db-shm');
const keepFile = path.join(sqliteDir, 'README.keep');
fs.writeFileSync(dbFile, 'dirty-sqlite-bytes');
fs.writeFileSync(walFile, 'dirty-wal-bytes');
fs.writeFileSync(shmFile, 'dirty-shm-bytes');
fs.writeFileSync(keepFile, 'preserve-me');

let handleClosed = false;
const targetModel = new TargetModel();
targetModel.activeSeriesSlug = 'gotham-trinity-crisis';
targetModel.targetBitList = [{ idx: 't1' }];
targetModel.targetBits = { t1: { idx: 't1' } };
(globalThis as any).ACTIVE_SERIES_SLUG = 'gotham-trinity-crisis';

const canonModel = new CanonModel();
canonModel.canonBitList = [{ idx: 'c1' }];
canonModel.canonBits = { c1: { idx: 'c1' } };
canonModel.factStandings.set('fact_1', { factId: 'fact_1' } as any);

const collectModel = new CollectModel();
collectModel.caboodleBitList = [{ idx: 'characters', dex: 0, bitList: ['char_batman'], bits: { char_batman: 0 } }];
collectModel.caboodleBits = { characters: 0 };

const dbModel = new DatabaseModel();
dbModel.activeDbHandle = { close: () => { handleClosed = true; } };
dbModel.activeDbPath = dbFile;
dbModel.databaseBitList = [{ idx: 'd1' }];
dbModel.databaseBits = { d1: { idx: 'd1' } };

const tRes = await resetTarget(targetModel);
const cRes = await resetCanon(canonModel);
const colRes = await resetCollect(collectModel);
const dRes = await resetDatabase(dbModel, { idx: 'reset-db', src: tmpDir });

if (tRes.activeSeriesSlug !== null || tRes.targetBitList.length !== 0 || Object.keys(tRes.targetBits).length !== 0 || (globalThis as any).ACTIVE_SERIES_SLUG !== undefined) {
  console.error(':: [FAIL] TargetModel contamination remained after resetTarget');
  process.exit(1);
}
if (cRes.canonBitList.length !== 0 || Object.keys(cRes.canonBits).length !== 0 || cRes.factStandings.size !== 0) {
  console.error(':: [FAIL] CanonModel contamination remained after resetCanon');
  process.exit(1);
}
if (colRes.caboodleBitList.length !== 0 || Object.keys(colRes.caboodleBits).length !== 0) {
  console.error(':: [FAIL] CollectModel contamination remained after resetCollect');
  process.exit(1);
}
if (!handleClosed || dRes.activeDbHandle !== null || dRes.activeDbPath !== null || fs.existsSync(dbFile) || fs.existsSync(walFile) || fs.existsSync(shmFile) || !fs.existsSync(keepFile)) {
  console.error(':: [FAIL] DatabaseModel handle close or SQLite sidecar purge failed');
  process.exit(1);
}
fs.rmSync(tmpDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
console.log('>> [OK] All 4 stateful models and SQLite sidecar cleanup verified clean.');
"
```

- *Pass Criterion:* Vitest exits `0` with 100% passing assertions; all 4 models return to exact primordial constructor defaults; open SQLite handles close cleanly prior to unlinking `.local.db` / `-wal` / `-shm` sidecars while preserving non-SQLite files.

  - Wire `SCAN_LORE_BLAST_RADIUS` and `EXECUTE_TABULA_RASA_LORE` in `packages/299.lore/00.lore.unit/lore.reduce.ts` (using `clone(model)`) and re-export `scanLoreBlastRadius` and `executeTabulaRasaLore` from `packages/299.lore/00.lore.unit/lore.buzzer.ts`.
3. **Task 3.3 — Colocated Vitest Verification Suite (`packages/299.lore/00.lore.unit/test/tabulaRasaDiskPurge.test.ts`):**
  - Create a pure 7-bit ASCII Vitest suite inside an isolated `os.tmpdir()` sandbox testing:
    1. Non-destructive `scanLoreBlastRadius` accurately counting `series/` folders, `.md` dossiers, `compiled/bible-state.json`, and `topology.manifest.json` files without modifying disk.
    2. Fail-closed token rejection when `executeTabulaRasaLore` is invoked with missing or invalid Triple-Lock confirmation tokens (asserting all files remain intact on disk and `aborted === true`).
    3. Full atomic obliteration when `executeTabulaRasaLore` is invoked with all three valid confirmation tokens (`CONFIRM_1_OF_3`, `CONFIRM_2_OF_3`, `CONFIRM_3_OF_3`): asserting `series/*` (including `_retired/`) and `compiled/*` are wiped while empty `series/` and `compiled/` containers remain, protected paths (`data/directive/day-001/test.md`, `.env`) remain untouched, `RESET_TARGET`, `RESET_CANON`, `RESET_COLLECT`, and `RESET_DATABASE` are dispatched via `ste.hunt`, and `cns00` receives pure 7-bit ASCII receipts.

##### IV. CONCRETE FILE IMPLEMENTATIONS

###### 1. `packages/299.lore/00.lore.unit/buz/tabulaRasa.buzz.ts` *(CREATE)*

```typescript
/**
 * packages/299.lore/00.lore.unit/buz/tabulaRasa.buzz.ts
 * Pre-flight blast-radius inventory scanner (Task 3.1) and atomic disk directory
 * obliteration buzzer (Task 3.2) for 299.lore Tabula Rasa Void-State resets.
 * Pure 7-bit ASCII compliance; zero bare stdout/stderr calls.
 */
import * as fs from 'node:fs'
import * as path from 'node:path'
import type { LoreModel } from '../lore.model.js'
import type LoreBit from '../fce/lore.bit.js'
import type State from '../../99.core/state.js'
import {
  GENESIS_MERKLE_ROOT_64,
  TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_1,
  TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_2,
  TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_3,
  type LoreBlastRadiusInventory,
  type LorePurgedCounts,
  type LoreTabulaRasaCascadeOrigin,
  type LoreTabulaRasaReceipt,
  type LoreTabulaRasaRequestBit,
} from '../../38.types.unit/fce/tabulaRasa.interface.js'
import {
  assertSafeResetTarget,
  resolveResetLoreRoot,
} from './resetGuard.buzz.js'

const UPDATE_CONSOLE = '[Console action] Update Console'
const RESET_TARGET = '[Target action] Reset Target'
const RESET_CANON = '[Canon action] Reset Canon'
const RESET_COLLECT = '[Collect action] Reset Collect'
const RESET_DATABASE = '[Database action] Reset Database'

function sanitizeAscii(raw: string): string {
  return String(raw || '')
    .replace(/[^\x20-\x7E]/g, '')
    .trim()
}

async function emitLoreResetTelemetry(
  ste: State | undefined,
  lines: readonly string[],
): Promise<void> {
  const lib = (globalThis as any).LIBRARY || (global as any).LIBRARY
  for (const rawLine of lines) {
    const clean = sanitizeAscii(rawLine)
    if (lib && typeof lib.hunt === 'function') {
      await lib.hunt(UPDATE_CONSOLE, { idx: 'cns00', src: clean }).catch(() => {})
    } else if (ste && typeof ste.hunt === 'function') {
      await ste.hunt(UPDATE_CONSOLE, { idx: 'cns00', src: clean }).catch(() => {})
    }
  }
}

function collectFilesRecursive(dirPath: string): string[] {
  if (!fs.existsSync(dirPath)) {
    return []
  }
  const files: string[] = []
  const entries = fs.readdirSync(dirPath, { withFileTypes: true })
  for (const entry of entries) {
    if (entry.name === '.gitkeep') {
      continue
    }
    const fullPath = path.join(dirPath, entry.name)
    if (entry.isDirectory()) {
      files.push(...collectFilesRecursive(fullPath))
    } else if (entry.isFile()) {
      files.push(fullPath)
    }
  }
  return files
}

function isScratchpadFileName(filename: string): boolean {
  return (
    filename.startsWith('.tmp_') ||
    filename.includes('.tmp_') ||
    filename.endsWith('.tmp')
  )
}

/**
 * Task 3.1: Non-destructive pre-flight scanner evaluating series/, compiled/,
 * lore/errata.json, and orphaned .tmp_* scratchpads prior to reset authorization.
 */
export async function scanLoreBlastRadius(
  repoRootInput?: string,
  ste?: State,
): Promise<LoreBlastRadiusInventory> {
  const repoRoot = resolveResetLoreRoot(repoRootInput || process.cwd())

  // 1. Scan series/ directories and Markdown dossiers (including _retired/)
  const seriesRoot = path.join(repoRoot, 'series')
  const discoveredSeriesSlugs: string[] = []
  let markdownDossiers = 0

  if (fs.existsSync(seriesRoot)) {
    const seriesEntries = fs.readdirSync(seriesRoot, { withFileTypes: true })
    for (const entry of seriesEntries) {
      if (
        entry.isDirectory() &&
        !entry.name.startsWith('.') &&
        entry.name !== '.gitkeep'
      ) {
        discoveredSeriesSlugs.push(entry.name)
      }
    }
    const allSeriesFiles = collectFilesRecursive(seriesRoot)
    for (const file of allSeriesFiles) {
      if (file.toLowerCase().endsWith('.md')) {
        markdownDossiers++
      }
    }
  }
  discoveredSeriesSlugs.sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b))

  // 2. Scan compiled/ bible-state.json and topology.manifest.json projections
  const compiledRoot = path.join(repoRoot, 'compiled')
  let compiledBibleManifests = 0
  let topologyManifests = 0

  if (fs.existsSync(compiledRoot)) {
    const allCompiledFiles = collectFilesRecursive(compiledRoot)
    for (const file of allCompiledFiles) {
      const base = path.basename(file).toLowerCase()
      if (base === 'bible-state.json') {
        compiledBibleManifests++
      } else if (base === 'topology.manifest.json') {
        topologyManifests++
      }
    }
  }

  // 3. Scan lore/errata.json and errata ledgers
  const loreDir = path.join(repoRoot, 'lore')
  let errataFiles = 0
  if (fs.existsSync(loreDir)) {
    const loreEntries = fs.readdirSync(loreDir, { withFileTypes: true })
    for (const entry of loreEntries) {
      if (!entry.isFile()) continue
      const lower = entry.name.toLowerCase()
      if (
        lower === 'errata.json' ||
        (lower.startsWith('canon_errata_') && lower.endsWith('.json'))
      ) {
        errataFiles++
      }
    }
  }

  // 4. Scan orphaned scratchpads across runtime folders
  let scratchpadFiles = 0
  const scratchpadScanDirs = [
    seriesRoot,
    compiledRoot,
    loreDir,
    path.join(repoRoot, 'data', 'lockfile'),
  ]
  for (const scanDir of scratchpadScanDirs) {
    const files = collectFilesRecursive(scanDir)
    for (const file of files) {
      if (isScratchpadFileName(path.basename(file))) {
        scratchpadFiles++
      }
    }
  }

  const counts: LorePurgedCounts = {
    seriesDirectories: discoveredSeriesSlugs.length,
    markdownDossiers,
    compiledBibleManifests,
    topologyManifests,
    errataFiles,
    scratchpadFiles,
  }

  const targetPaths = [
    path.join(repoRoot, 'series'),
    path.join(repoRoot, 'compiled'),
    path.join(repoRoot, 'lore', 'errata.json'),
  ]

  const inventory: LoreBlastRadiusInventory = {
    repoRoot,
    counts,
    discoveredSeriesSlugs,
    targetPaths,
    scannedAtIso: new Date().toISOString(),
  }

  const telemetryLines = [
    '>> ============================================================',
    '>> [LORE:BLAST_RADIUS] PRE-FLIGHT VOID-STATE INVENTORY',
    `>> Target Root       : ${repoRoot}`,
    `>> Series Folders    : ${counts.seriesDirectories} (${discoveredSeriesSlugs.join(', ') || 'NONE'})`,
    `>> Markdown Dossiers : ${counts.markdownDossiers} .md file(s)`,
    `>> Compiled Manifests: ${counts.compiledBibleManifests} bible-state.json | ${counts.topologyManifests} topology`,
    `>> Errata & Temp     : ${counts.errataFiles} errata | ${counts.scratchpadFiles} scratchpad(s)`,
    '>> ============================================================',
  ]
  await emitLoreResetTelemetry(ste, telemetryLines)

  return inventory
}

/**
 * Task 3.2: Atomic disk directory obliteration buzzer for 299.lore.
 * Enforces Triple-Lock confirmation tokens, validates every target via
 * assertSafeResetTarget, wipes series/* and compiled/* while preserving
 * empty root containers, and cascades in-memory resets to 34.target,
 * 41.canon, 97.collect, and 07.database.
 */
export const executeTabulaRasaLore = async (
  cpy: LoreModel,
  bal?: LoreBit,
  ste?: State,
): Promise<LoreModel> => {
  const datObj = (bal?.dat || {}) as Record<string, any>
  const req: LoreTabulaRasaRequestBit =
    (datObj.tabulaRasaRequest as LoreTabulaRasaRequestBit) || datObj

  const repoRoot = resolveResetLoreRoot(
    req.repoRoot || bal?.src || process.cwd(),
  )
  const cascadeOrigin: LoreTabulaRasaCascadeOrigin =
    req.cascadeOrigin || 'LOCAL_TUI'

  const zeroCounts: LorePurgedCounts = {
    seriesDirectories: 0,
    markdownDossiers: 0,
    compiledBibleManifests: 0,
    topologyManifests: 0,
    errataFiles: 0,
    scratchpadFiles: 0,
  }

  // 1. Fail-closed Triple-Lock confirmation token verification
  let abortStage: 1 | 2 | 3 | undefined
  if (req.confirmToken1 !== TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_1) {
    abortStage = 1
  } else if (req.confirmToken2 !== TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_2) {
    abortStage = 2
  } else if (req.confirmToken3 !== TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_3) {
    abortStage = 3
  }

  if (abortStage !== undefined) {
    const abortLine = `:: [TABULA_RASA:ABORTED] Triple-Lock verification failed at Stage ${abortStage}/3. Disk untouched.`
    await emitLoreResetTelemetry(ste, [abortLine])

    const abortedReceipt: LoreTabulaRasaReceipt = {
      ok: false,
      aborted: true,
      abortStage,
      voidStateVerified: false,
      cascadeOrigin,
      purgedCounts: zeroCounts,
      unitsResetList: [],
      merkleRootHex: GENESIS_MERKLE_ROOT_64,
      dispatchLockEngaged: true,
      timestampIso: new Date().toISOString(),
      receipts: [abortLine],
    }

    if (bal?.slv) {
      bal.slv({
        lorBit: {
          idx: 'execute-tabula-rasa-lore-aborted',
          val: 0,
          dat: abortedReceipt,
        },
      })
    }
    return cpy
  }

  // 2. Capture pre-purge blast-radius metrics
  const inventory = await scanLoreBlastRadius(repoRoot, ste)
  const receipts: string[] = [
    `>> [TABULA_RASA:INIT] Executing 299.lore atomic disk purge (origin=${cascadeOrigin})...`,
  ]

  // 3. Validate root purge targets through fail-closed perimeter guard
  const seriesRoot = assertSafeResetTarget(
    repoRoot,
    path.join(repoRoot, 'series'),
  )
  const compiledRoot = assertSafeResetTarget(
    repoRoot,
    path.join(repoRoot, 'compiled'),
  )

  // 4. Atomically obliterate all subdirectories & files inside series/* (preserving .gitkeep)
  let purgedSeriesDirs = 0
  if (fs.existsSync(seriesRoot)) {
    const entries = fs.readdirSync(seriesRoot, { withFileTypes: true })
    for (const entry of entries) {
      if (entry.name === '.gitkeep') {
        continue
      }
      const childPath = path.join(seriesRoot, entry.name)
      const safeChild = assertSafeResetTarget(repoRoot, childPath)
      fs.rmSync(safeChild, { recursive: true, force: true })
      if (entry.isDirectory()) {
        purgedSeriesDirs++
      }
    }
  } else {
    fs.mkdirSync(seriesRoot, { recursive: true })
  }

  receipts.push(
    `>> [TABULA_RASA:SERIES] Purged ${purgedSeriesDirs} series directories (${inventory.counts.markdownDossiers} Markdown dossiers) [OK]`,
  )

  // 5. Atomically obliterate all subdirectories & files inside compiled/* (preserving .gitkeep)
  if (fs.existsSync(compiledRoot)) {
    const entries = fs.readdirSync(compiledRoot, { withFileTypes: true })
    for (const entry of entries) {
      if (entry.name === '.gitkeep') {
        continue
      }
      const childPath = path.join(compiledRoot, entry.name)
      const safeChild = assertSafeResetTarget(repoRoot, childPath)
      fs.rmSync(safeChild, { recursive: true, force: true })
    }
  } else {
    fs.mkdirSync(compiledRoot, { recursive: true })
  }

  receipts.push(
    `>> [TABULA_RASA:COMPILED] Purged ${inventory.counts.compiledBibleManifests} bible-state.json and ${inventory.counts.topologyManifests} topology manifests [OK]`,
  )

  // 6. Cascade in-memory unit resets across 34.target, 41.canon, 97.collect, and 07.database
  const unitsResetList: string[] = [
    '00.lore.unit',
    '34.target.unit',
    '41.canon.unit',
    '97.collect.unit',
    '07.database.unit',
  ]

  cpy.idx = '00.lore'
  cpy.loreBitList = []
  cpy.loreBits = {}

  if (ste && typeof ste.hunt === 'function') {
    await ste.hunt(RESET_TARGET, { idx: 'tabula-rasa-target' }).catch(() => {})
    await ste.hunt(RESET_CANON, { idx: 'tabula-rasa-canon' }).catch(() => {})
    await ste.hunt(RESET_COLLECT, { idx: 'tabula-rasa-collect' }).catch(() => {})
    await ste
      .hunt(RESET_DATABASE, { idx: 'tabula-rasa-database', src: repoRoot })
      .catch(() => {})
  }

  receipts.push(
    '>> [TABULA_RASA:MEMORY] Re-initialized 34.target, 41.canon, 97.collect, and 07.database [OK]',
  )

  const purgedCounts: LorePurgedCounts = {
    seriesDirectories: purgedSeriesDirs,
    markdownDossiers: inventory.counts.markdownDossiers,
    compiledBibleManifests: inventory.counts.compiledBibleManifests,
    topologyManifests: inventory.counts.topologyManifests,
    errataFiles: 0,
    scratchpadFiles: 0,
  }

  const finalReceipt: LoreTabulaRasaReceipt = {
    ok: true,
    aborted: false,
    voidStateVerified: true,
    cascadeOrigin,
    purgedCounts,
    unitsResetList,
    merkleRootHex: GENESIS_MERKLE_ROOT_64,
    dispatchLockEngaged: true,
    timestampIso: new Date().toISOString(),
    receipts,
  }

  await emitLoreResetTelemetry(ste, receipts)

  if (bal?.slv) {
    bal.slv({
      lorBit: {
        idx: 'execute-tabula-rasa-lore-pass',
        val: 1,
        dat: finalReceipt,
      },
    })
  }

  return cpy
}
```

```bash
import * as fs from 'node:fs';

const tmpRoot = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'lore-phase3-audit-'));
fs.mkdirSync(path.join(tmpRoot, 'packages', '299.lore'), { recursive: true });
fs.mkdirSync(path.join(tmpRoot, 'series', 'gotham-trinity-crisis', 'characters', '_retired'), { recursive: true });
fs.mkdirSync(path.join(tmpRoot, 'compiled', 'gotham-trinity-crisis'), { recursive: true });
fs.mkdirSync(path.join(tmpRoot, 'data', 'directive', 'day-001'), { recursive: true });

fs.writeFileSync(path.join(tmpRoot, 'series', '.gitkeep'), '');
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(tmpRoot, 'series', 'gotham-trinity-crisis', 'series.config.json'), '{}');
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(tmpRoot, 'series', 'gotham-trinity-crisis', 'characters', 'char_wonder_woman.md'), '---\nid: char_wonder_woman\n---\n');
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(tmpRoot, 'series', 'gotham-trinity-crisis', 'characters', '_retired', 'char_old.md'), '---\nid: char_old\n---\n');
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(tmpRoot, 'compiled', '.gitkeep'), '');
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(tmpRoot, 'compiled', 'gotham-trinity-crisis', 'bible-state.json'), '{}');
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(tmpRoot, 'compiled', 'gotham-trinity-crisis', 'topology.manifest.json'), '{}');
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(tmpRoot, 'data', 'directive', 'day-001', 'protected.md'), 'protected');
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(tmpRoot, '.env'), 'SECRET=1');

// 1. Verify non-destructive blast-radius scan
const inv = await scanLoreBlastRadius(tmpRoot);
if (inv.counts.seriesDirectories !== 1 || inv.counts.markdownDossiers !== 2 || inv.counts.compiledBibleManifests !== 1 || inv.counts.topologyManifests !== 1) {
  console.error(':: [FAIL] Blast-radius scanner miscounted runtime artifacts: ' + JSON.stringify(inv.counts));
  process.exit(1);
}

// 2. Verify fail-closed rejection on partial Triple-Lock tokens
const huntedActions: string[] = [];
const mockState: any = {
  hunt: async (type: string) => {
    huntedActions.push(type);
    return {};
  }
};
const loreModel = new LoreModel();
loreModel.loreBitList = [{ idx: 'dirty-bit' }];
loreModel.loreBits = { 'dirty-bit': { idx: 'dirty-bit' } };

let abortedResult: any = null;
await executeTabulaRasaLore(loreModel, {
  idx: 'audit-abort',
  src: tmpRoot,
  dat: { confirmToken1: TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_1, confirmToken2: TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_2 },
  slv: (res: any) => { abortedResult = res.lorBit; }
}, mockState);

if (!abortedResult || abortedResult.idx !== 'execute-tabula-rasa-lore-aborted' || !fs.existsSync(path.join(tmpRoot, 'series', 'gotham-trinity-crisis'))) {
  console.error(':: [FAIL] executeTabulaRasaLore failed to abort cleanly when Stage 3 token was missing!');
  process.exit(1);
}

// 3. Verify full atomic purge with all 3 valid tokens
let passResult: any = null;
const cleanedModel = await executeTabulaRasaLore(loreModel, {
  idx: 'audit-pass',
  src: tmpRoot,
  dat: {
    confirmToken1: TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_1,
    confirmToken2: TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_2,
    confirmToken3: TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_3
  },
  slv: (res: any) => { passResult = res.lorBit; }
}, mockState);

if (!passResult || passResult.idx !== 'execute-tabula-rasa-lore-pass' || passResult.val !== 1) {
  console.error(':: [FAIL] executeTabulaRasaLore did not resolve pass receipt');
  process.exit(1);
}
if (fs.existsSync(path.join(tmpRoot, 'series', 'gotham-trinity-crisis')) || fs.existsSync(path.join(tmpRoot, 'compiled', 'gotham-trinity-crisis'))) {
  console.error(':: [FAIL] series/* or compiled/* directories survived atomic obliteration!');
  process.exit(1);
}
if (!fs.existsSync(path.join(tmpRoot, 'series', '.gitkeep')) || !fs.existsSync(path.join(tmpRoot, 'compiled', '.gitkeep')) || !fs.existsSync(path.join(tmpRoot, 'data', 'directive', 'day-001', 'protected.md')) || !fs.existsSync(path.join(tmpRoot, '.env'))) {
  console.error(':: [FAIL] Protected files or .gitkeep anchors were damaged!');
  process.exit(1);
}
const expectedCascade = ['[Target action] Reset Target', '[Canon action] Reset Canon', '[Collect action] Reset Collect', '[Database action] Reset Database'];
for (const act of expectedCascade) {
  if (!huntedActions.includes(act)) {
    console.error(':: [FAIL] Missing downstream unit reset cascade action: ' + act);
    process.exit(1);
  }
}
if (cleanedModel.loreBitList.length !== 0 || Object.keys(cleanedModel.loreBits).length !== 0) {
  console.error(':: [FAIL] LoreModel state was not cleared');
  process.exit(1);
}

fs.rmSync(tmpRoot, { recursive: true, force: true });
console.log('>> [OK] Task 3.1 scanner and Task 3.2 atomic disk obliteration verified clean.');
"
```

###### 1. `packages/299.lore/32.status.unit/buz/voidStateScan.buzz.ts` *(UPDATE)*

Preserve `scanVoidState` and update `voidStateScan` so Void-State HUD lines and `GENESIS_MERKLE_ROOT_64` metadata are stamped deterministically when `result.isVoidState === true`:

```typescript
import * as fs from 'node:fs'
import * as path from 'node:path'
import type State from '../../99.core/state.js'
import type { StatusModel } from '../status.model.js'
import type StatusBit from '../fce/status.bit.js'
import { resolveRepoRoot } from '../../00.lore.unit/buz/lore.buzz.js'
import type { VoidScanResultBit } from '../fce/voidScan.bit.js'
import { GENESIS_MERKLE_ROOT_64 } from '../../38.types.unit/fce/tabulaRasa.interface.js'

/**
 * Pure filesystem evaluator scanning <repoRoot>/series for directories
 * possessing a valid, non-empty, and readable series.config.json manifest.
 */
export const scanVoidState = (repoRootInput?: string): VoidScanResultBit => {
  const repoRoot = repoRootInput || resolveRepoRoot()
  const seriesPath = path.join(repoRoot, 'series')

  if (!fs.existsSync(seriesPath)) {
    return {
      isVoidState: true,
      discoveredSlugs: [],
      repoRoot,
      seriesPath,
    }
  }

  const entries = fs.readdirSync(seriesPath, { withFileTypes: true })
  const discoveredSlugs: string[] = []

  for (const entry of entries) {
    if (entry.isDirectory()) {
      const configPath = path.join(seriesPath, entry.name, 'series.config.json')
      if (fs.existsSync(configPath)) {
        try {
          const raw = fs.readFileSync(configPath, 'utf-8')
          if (raw.trim().length > 0) {
            const parsed = JSON.parse(raw)
            if (parsed && typeof parsed === 'object' && !Array.isArray(parsed)) {
              discoveredSlugs.push(entry.name)
            }
          }
        } catch {
          // Unparseable or empty manifests are ignored; directory does not qualify as sealed
        }
      }
    }
  }

  discoveredSlugs.sort((a, b) => (a < b ? -1 : a > b ? 1 : 0))

  return {
    isVoidState: discoveredSlugs.length === 0,
    discoveredSlugs,
    repoRoot,
    seriesPath,
  }
}

/**
 * Reactive Buzzer handler for ActSts.EVALUATE_VOID_STATE.
 * Synchronizes StatusModel HUD lines, dispatch lock, border color, and genesis Merkle root.
 */
export const voidStateScan = async (
  cpy: StatusModel,
  bal: StatusBit,
  _ste?: State,
): Promise<StatusModel> => {
  const targetRoot = bal?.src || undefined
  const result = scanVoidState(targetRoot)

  cpy.isVoidState = result.isVoidState
  cpy.discoveredSlugs = [...result.discoveredSlugs]
  cpy.dispatchLockEngaged = result.isVoidState
  cpy.activeBorderColor = result.isVoidState ? 'amber' : 'green'

  if (result.isVoidState) {
    cpy.hudLines = [
      'ACTIVE SERIES : [NONE (UNBOUND)]',
      'CANON STATUS  : VOID STATE (T0=$0.00)',
      `ROOT MERKLE   : ${GENESIS_MERKLE_ROOT_64}`,
      'DISPATCH LOCK : [!] ENGAGED',
    ]
  } else {
    cpy.hudLines = [
      `ACTIVE SERIES : [${result.discoveredSlugs.join(', ')}]`,
      'CANON STATUS  : SEALED CORPUS ACTIVE',
      `SERIES COUNT  : ${result.discoveredSlugs.length}`,
      'DISPATCH LOCK : [OK] DISENGAGED',
    ]
  }

  if (bal?.slv) {
    bal.slv({
      statusBit: {
        idx: 'void-state-scan',
        val: result.isVoidState ? 1 : 0,
        dat: {
          ...result,
          merkleRootHex: result.isVoidState ? GENESIS_MERKLE_ROOT_64 : undefined,
          hudLines: [...cpy.hudLines],
        },
      },
    })
  }

  return cpy
}
```

###### 2. `packages/299.lore/00.lore.unit/buz/tabulaRasa.buzz.ts` *(UPDATE)*

Add `purgeErrataAndScratchpads` (Task 3.3) and wire both `purgeErrataAndScratchpads` and `ActSts.EVALUATE_VOID_STATE` synchronization (Task 3.4) inside `executeTabulaRasaLore`:

```typescript
/**
 * packages/299.lore/00.lore.unit/buz/tabulaRasa.buzz.ts
 * Pre-flight blast-radius inventory scanner (Task 3.1), atomic disk directory
 * obliteration buzzer (Task 3.2), errata/scratchpad purge (Task 3.3), and
 * status unit void-state synchronization (Task 3.4) for 299.lore.
 * Pure 7-bit ASCII compliance; zero bare stdout/stderr calls.
 */
import * as fs from 'node:fs'
import * as path from 'node:path'
import type { LoreModel } from '../lore.model.js'
import type LoreBit from '../fce/lore.bit.js'
import type State from '../../99.core/state.js'
import {
  GENESIS_MERKLE_ROOT_64,
  TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_1,
  TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_2,
  TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_3,
  type LoreBlastRadiusInventory,
  type LorePurgedCounts,
  type LoreTabulaRasaCascadeOrigin,
  type LoreTabulaRasaReceipt,
  type LoreTabulaRasaRequestBit,
} from '../../38.types.unit/fce/tabulaRasa.interface.js'
import {
  assertSafeResetTarget,
  resolveResetLoreRoot,
} from './resetGuard.buzz.js'
import { scanVoidState } from '../../32.status.unit/buz/voidStateScan.buzz.js'

const UPDATE_CONSOLE = '[Console action] Update Console'
const RESET_TARGET = '[Target action] Reset Target'
const RESET_CANON = '[Canon action] Reset Canon'
const RESET_COLLECT = '[Collect action] Reset Collect'
const RESET_DATABASE = '[Database action] Reset Database'
const EVALUATE_VOID_STATE = '[Status action] Evaluate Void State'

function sanitizeAscii(raw: string): string {
  return String(raw || '')
    .replace(/[^\x20-\x7E]/g, '')
    .trim()
}

async function emitLoreResetTelemetry(
  ste: State | undefined,
  lines: readonly string[],
): Promise<void> {
  const lib = (globalThis as any).LIBRARY || (global as any).LIBRARY
  for (const rawLine of lines) {
    const clean = sanitizeAscii(rawLine)
    if (lib && typeof lib.hunt === 'function') {
      await lib.hunt(UPDATE_CONSOLE, { idx: 'cns00', src: clean }).catch(() => {})
    } else if (ste && typeof ste.hunt === 'function') {
      await ste.hunt(UPDATE_CONSOLE, { idx: 'cns00', src: clean }).catch(() => {})
    }
  }
}

function collectFilesRecursive(dirPath: string): string[] {
  if (!fs.existsSync(dirPath)) {
    return []
  }
  const files: string[] = []
  const entries = fs.readdirSync(dirPath, { withFileTypes: true })
  for (const entry of entries) {
    if (entry.name === '.gitkeep') {
      continue
    }
    const fullPath = path.join(dirPath, entry.name)
    if (entry.isDirectory()) {
      files.push(...collectFilesRecursive(fullPath))
    } else if (entry.isFile()) {
      files.push(fullPath)
    }
  }
  return files
}

function isScratchpadFileName(filename: string): boolean {
  return (
    filename.startsWith('.tmp_') ||
    filename.includes('.tmp_') ||
    filename.endsWith('.tmp')
  )
}

function isErrataLedgerFileName(filename: string): boolean {
  const lower = filename.toLowerCase()
  return (
    lower === 'errata.json' ||
    (lower.startsWith('canon_errata_') && lower.endsWith('.json'))
  )
}

/**
 * Task 3.3: Purges lore/errata.json (and canon_errata_*.json files) as well as
 * orphaned .tmp_* conflict or editor scratchpad files across runtime folders.
 */
export async function purgeErrataAndScratchpads(
  repoRootInput?: string,
  ste?: State,
): Promise<{ errataFiles: number; scratchpadFiles: number }> {
  const repoRoot = resolveResetLoreRoot(repoRootInput || process.cwd())
  let errataFiles = 0
  let scratchpadFiles = 0

  // 1. Purge errata ledger files inside <repoRoot>/lore/
  const loreDir = path.join(repoRoot, 'lore')
  if (fs.existsSync(loreDir)) {
    const loreEntries = fs.readdirSync(loreDir, { withFileTypes: true })
    for (const entry of loreEntries) {
      if (!entry.isFile() || entry.name === '.gitkeep') continue
      if (isErrataLedgerFileName(entry.name)) {
        const candidate = path.join(loreDir, entry.name)
        const safePath = assertSafeResetTarget(repoRoot, candidate)
        fs.rmSync(safePath, { force: true })
        errataFiles++
      }
    }
  }

  // 2. Sweep and unlink orphaned scratchpad files across runtime directories
  const scratchpadScanDirs = [
    path.join(repoRoot, 'series'),
    path.join(repoRoot, 'compiled'),
    loreDir,
    path.join(repoRoot, 'data', 'lockfile'),
  ]

  for (const scanDir of scratchpadScanDirs) {
    const files = collectFilesRecursive(scanDir)
    for (const file of files) {
      if (isScratchpadFileName(path.basename(file))) {
        const safeScratchpad = assertSafeResetTarget(repoRoot, file)
        fs.rmSync(safeScratchpad, { force: true })
        scratchpadFiles++
      }
    }
  }

  await emitLoreResetTelemetry(ste, [
    `>> [TABULA_RASA:ERRATA] Purged ${errataFiles} errata ledger(s) and ${scratchpadFiles} scratchpad file(s) [OK]`,
  ])

  return { errataFiles, scratchpadFiles }
}

/**
 * Task 3.1: Non-destructive pre-flight scanner evaluating series/, compiled/,
 * lore/errata.json, and orphaned .tmp_* scratchpads prior to reset authorization.
 */
export async function scanLoreBlastRadius(
  repoRootInput?: string,
  ste?: State,
): Promise<LoreBlastRadiusInventory> {
  const repoRoot = resolveResetLoreRoot(repoRootInput || process.cwd())

  const seriesRoot = path.join(repoRoot, 'series')
  const discoveredSeriesSlugs: string[] = []
  let markdownDossiers = 0

  if (fs.existsSync(seriesRoot)) {
    const seriesEntries = fs.readdirSync(seriesRoot, { withFileTypes: true })
    for (const entry of seriesEntries) {
      if (
        entry.isDirectory() &&
        !entry.name.startsWith('.') &&
        entry.name !== '.gitkeep'
      ) {
        discoveredSeriesSlugs.push(entry.name)
      }
    }
    const allSeriesFiles = collectFilesRecursive(seriesRoot)
    for (const file of allSeriesFiles) {
      if (file.toLowerCase().endsWith('.md')) {
        markdownDossiers++
      }
    }
  }
  discoveredSeriesSlugs.sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b))

  const compiledRoot = path.join(repoRoot, 'compiled')
  let compiledBibleManifests = 0
  let topologyManifests = 0

  if (fs.existsSync(compiledRoot)) {
    const allCompiledFiles = collectFilesRecursive(compiledRoot)
    for (const file of allCompiledFiles) {
      const base = path.basename(file).toLowerCase()
      if (base === 'bible-state.json') {
        compiledBibleManifests++
      } else if (base === 'topology.manifest.json') {
        topologyManifests++
      }
    }
  }

  const loreDir = path.join(repoRoot, 'lore')
  let errataFiles = 0
  if (fs.existsSync(loreDir)) {
    const loreEntries = fs.readdirSync(loreDir, { withFileTypes: true })
    for (const entry of loreEntries) {
      if (!entry.isFile()) continue
      if (isErrataLedgerFileName(entry.name)) {
        errataFiles++
      }
    }
  }

  let scratchpadFiles = 0
  const scratchpadScanDirs = [
    seriesRoot,
    compiledRoot,
    loreDir,
    path.join(repoRoot, 'data', 'lockfile'),
  ]
  for (const scanDir of scratchpadScanDirs) {
    const files = collectFilesRecursive(scanDir)
    for (const file of files) {
      if (isScratchpadFileName(path.basename(file))) {
        scratchpadFiles++
      }
    }
  }

  const counts: LorePurgedCounts = {
    seriesDirectories: discoveredSeriesSlugs.length,
    markdownDossiers,
    compiledBibleManifests,
    topologyManifests,
    errataFiles,
    scratchpadFiles,
  }

  const targetPaths = [
    path.join(repoRoot, 'series'),
    path.join(repoRoot, 'compiled'),
    path.join(repoRoot, 'lore', 'errata.json'),
  ]

  const inventory: LoreBlastRadiusInventory = {
    repoRoot,
    counts,
    discoveredSeriesSlugs,
    targetPaths,
    scannedAtIso: new Date().toISOString(),
  }

  const telemetryLines = [
    '>> ============================================================',
    '>> [LORE:BLAST_RADIUS] PRE-FLIGHT VOID-STATE INVENTORY',
    `>> Target Root       : ${repoRoot}`,
    `>> Series Folders    : ${counts.seriesDirectories} (${discoveredSeriesSlugs.join(', ') || 'NONE'})`,
    `>> Markdown Dossiers : ${counts.markdownDossiers} .md file(s)`,
    `>> Compiled Manifests: ${counts.compiledBibleManifests} bible-state.json | ${counts.topologyManifests} topology`,
    `>> Errata & Temp     : ${counts.errataFiles} errata | ${counts.scratchpadFiles} scratchpad(s)`,
    '>> ============================================================',
  ]
  await emitLoreResetTelemetry(ste, telemetryLines)

  return inventory
}

/**
 * Tasks 3.2, 3.3 & 3.4: Complete local Void-State purge orchestrator.
 * Enforces Triple-Lock tokens, obliterates series/* and compiled/*, purges
 * errata ledgers and orphaned scratchpads, resets stateful BEE units, and
 * synchronizes 32.status.unit to Posture A Cold-Start Byte-0 Void State.
 */
export const executeTabulaRasaLore = async (
  cpy: LoreModel,
  bal?: LoreBit,
  ste?: State,
): Promise<LoreModel> => {
  const datObj = (bal?.dat || {}) as Record<string, any>
  const req: LoreTabulaRasaRequestBit =
    (datObj.tabulaRasaRequest as LoreTabulaRasaRequestBit) || datObj

  const repoRoot = resolveResetLoreRoot(
    req.repoRoot || bal?.src || process.cwd(),
  )
  const cascadeOrigin: LoreTabulaRasaCascadeOrigin =
    req.cascadeOrigin || 'LOCAL_TUI'

  const zeroCounts: LorePurgedCounts = {
    seriesDirectories: 0,
    markdownDossiers: 0,
    compiledBibleManifests: 0,
    topologyManifests: 0,
    errataFiles: 0,
    scratchpadFiles: 0,
  }

  let abortStage: 1 | 2 | 3 | undefined
  if (req.confirmToken1 !== TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_1) {
    abortStage = 1
  } else if (req.confirmToken2 !== TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_2) {
    abortStage = 2
  } else if (req.confirmToken3 !== TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_3) {
    abortStage = 3
  }

  if (abortStage !== undefined) {
    const abortLine = `:: [TABULA_RASA:ABORTED] Triple-Lock verification failed at Stage ${abortStage}/3. Disk untouched.`
    await emitLoreResetTelemetry(ste, [abortLine])

    const abortedReceipt: LoreTabulaRasaReceipt = {
      ok: false,
      aborted: true,
      abortStage,
      voidStateVerified: false,
      cascadeOrigin,
      purgedCounts: zeroCounts,
      unitsResetList: [],
      merkleRootHex: GENESIS_MERKLE_ROOT_64,
      dispatchLockEngaged: true,
      timestampIso: new Date().toISOString(),
      receipts: [abortLine],
    }

    if (bal?.slv) {
      bal.slv({
        lorBit: {
          idx: 'execute-tabula-rasa-lore-aborted',
          val: 0,
          dat: abortedReceipt,
        },
      })
    }
    return cpy
  }

  const inventory = await scanLoreBlastRadius(repoRoot, ste)
  const receipts: string[] = [
    `>> [TABULA_RASA:INIT] Executing 299.lore atomic disk purge (origin=${cascadeOrigin})...`,
  ]

  // Task 3.3: Purge errata ledgers and orphaned scratchpads across runtime dirs
  const errataPurgeStats = await purgeErrataAndScratchpads(repoRoot, ste)
  receipts.push(
    `>> [TABULA_RASA:ERRATA] Purged ${errataPurgeStats.errataFiles} errata ledger(s) and ${errataPurgeStats.scratchpadFiles} scratchpad file(s) [OK]`,
  )

  const seriesRoot = assertSafeResetTarget(
    repoRoot,
    path.join(repoRoot, 'series'),
  )
  const compiledRoot = assertSafeResetTarget(
    repoRoot,
    path.join(repoRoot, 'compiled'),
  )

  let purgedSeriesDirs = 0
  if (fs.existsSync(seriesRoot)) {
    const entries = fs.readdirSync(seriesRoot, { withFileTypes: true })
    for (const entry of entries) {
      if (entry.name === '.gitkeep') {
        continue
      }
      const childPath = path.join(seriesRoot, entry.name)
      const safeChild = assertSafeResetTarget(repoRoot, childPath)
      fs.rmSync(safeChild, { recursive: true, force: true })
      if (entry.isDirectory()) {
        purgedSeriesDirs++
      }
    }
  } else {
    fs.mkdirSync(seriesRoot, { recursive: true })
  }

  receipts.push(
    `>> [TABULA_RASA:SERIES] Purged ${purgedSeriesDirs} series directories (${inventory.counts.markdownDossiers} Markdown dossiers) [OK]`,
  )

  if (fs.existsSync(compiledRoot)) {
    const entries = fs.readdirSync(compiledRoot, { withFileTypes: true })
    for (const entry of entries) {
      if (entry.name === '.gitkeep') {
        continue
      }
      const childPath = path.join(compiledRoot, entry.name)
      const safeChild = assertSafeResetTarget(repoRoot, childPath)
      fs.rmSync(safeChild, { recursive: true, force: true })
    }
  } else {
    fs.mkdirSync(compiledRoot, { recursive: true })
  }

  receipts.push(
    `>> [TABULA_RASA:COMPILED] Purged ${inventory.counts.compiledBibleManifests} bible-state.json and ${inventory.counts.topologyManifests} topology manifests [OK]`,
  )

  const unitsResetList: string[] = [
    '00.lore.unit',
    '34.target.unit',
    '41.canon.unit',
    '97.collect.unit',
    '07.database.unit',
    '32.status.unit',
  ]

  cpy.idx = '00.lore'
  cpy.loreBitList = []
  cpy.loreBits = {}

  if (ste && typeof ste.hunt === 'function') {
    await ste.hunt(RESET_TARGET, { idx: 'tabula-rasa-target' }).catch(() => {})
    await ste.hunt(RESET_CANON, { idx: 'tabula-rasa-canon' }).catch(() => {})
    await ste.hunt(RESET_COLLECT, { idx: 'tabula-rasa-collect' }).catch(() => {})
    await ste
      .hunt(RESET_DATABASE, { idx: 'tabula-rasa-database', src: repoRoot })
      .catch(() => {})
    await ste
      .hunt(EVALUATE_VOID_STATE, {
        idx: 'tabula-rasa-void-sync',
        src: repoRoot,
      })
      .catch(() => {})
  }

  receipts.push(
    '>> [TABULA_RASA:MEMORY] Re-initialized 34.target, 41.canon, 97.collect, and 07.database [OK]',
  )

  // Task 3.4: Authoritative post-purge Void-State filesystem confirmation
  const postVoidScan = scanVoidState(repoRoot)
  const voidStateVerified =
    postVoidScan.isVoidState && postVoidScan.discoveredSlugs.length === 0

  if (voidStateVerified) {
    receipts.push(
      `>> [TABULA_RASA:HUD] Status unit synchronized -> VOID STATE (T0=$0.00) | MERKLE=${GENESIS_MERKLE_ROOT_64.slice(0, 8)}... | LOCK=ENGAGED [OK]`,
    )
  } else {
    receipts.push(
      `:: [TABULA_RASA:HUD] [FAIL] Residual series detected after purge: [${postVoidScan.discoveredSlugs.join(', ')}]`,
    )
  }

  const purgedCounts: LorePurgedCounts = {
    seriesDirectories: purgedSeriesDirs,
    markdownDossiers: inventory.counts.markdownDossiers,
    compiledBibleManifests: inventory.counts.compiledBibleManifests,
    topologyManifests: inventory.counts.topologyManifests,
    errataFiles: errataPurgeStats.errataFiles,
    scratchpadFiles: errataPurgeStats.scratchpadFiles,
  }

  const finalReceipt: LoreTabulaRasaReceipt = {
    ok: voidStateVerified,
    aborted: false,
    voidStateVerified,
    cascadeOrigin,
    purgedCounts,
    unitsResetList,
    merkleRootHex: GENESIS_MERKLE_ROOT_64,
    dispatchLockEngaged: true,
    timestampIso: new Date().toISOString(),
    receipts,
  }

  await emitLoreResetTelemetry(ste, receipts)

  if (bal?.slv) {
    bal.slv({
      lorBit: {
        idx: voidStateVerified
          ? 'execute-tabula-rasa-lore-pass'
          : 'execute-tabula-rasa-lore-fail',
        val: voidStateVerified ? 1 : -1,
        dat: finalReceipt,
      },
    })
  }

  return cpy
}
```

### **FUNCTIONAL MANDATE**

**User Story** It is 04:45 AM on the production floor of `000.goblin-lore`, and an autonomous Coding Agent has just finished exercising a full multi-episode simulation cycle for *Gotham Trinity Crisis*—appending post-broadcast retcons into `lore/errata.json`, staging temporary `.tmp_*` conflict and editor scratchpads during Human Save Gate resolution, and running `32.status.unit` with the active green HUD border unlocked (`isVoidState: false`, `dispatchLockEngaged: false`). Before starting the next end-to-end verification pass from a blank slate, the Showrunner triggers a Tabula Rasa Void-State reset. If `series/*` and `compiled/*` are wiped while `lore/errata.json` retains old retcon hashes, orphaned `.tmp_*` scratchpads linger in `data/lockfile/` or `lore/`, or `32.status.unit` still reports an active storyworld with an unlocked dispatch gate, the next run would launch with corrupted errata lineage and an unguarded status HUD. By implementing **Task 3.3 (Errata Ledger Scratchpad Purge Buzzer)** and **Task 3.4 (Synchronize Status Unit Void-State Evaluation)** in `packages/299.lore/00.lore.unit/` and `32.status.unit/`, `purgeErrataAndScratchpads` sweeps and unlinks `lore/errata.json`, `canon_errata_*.json`, and orphaned `.tmp_*` files through `assertSafeResetTarget`, while `executeTabulaRasaLore` chains `ActSts.EVALUATE_VOID_STATE` (`voidStateScan`) to re-arm `dispatchLockEngaged: true`, shift `activeBorderColor` back to `'amber'`, and stamp the 64-zero `GENESIS_MERKLE_ROOT_64` onto `StatusModel.hudLines` at **Posture A: Cold-Start Byte-0 Void State ($T_0 = \$0.00$)**.

**Strategic Goal** Supports the foundational pipeline objective of **Zero-Residue Errata Cleanup & Authoritative Status HUD Re-Arming at Stage $T_0 = \$0.00$**, guaranteeing that `000.goblin-lore` purges all append-only errata ledgers and temporary scratchpads while synchronizing `32.status.unit` back to **Posture A: Cold-Start Byte-0 Void State** with `dispatchLockEngaged: true`.

**Context & Value**

- **The Strategic Value:** Completes the local disk and HUD reset pipeline in `000.goblin-lore` by ensuring `lore/errata.json`, `canon_errata_*.json`, and orphaned `.tmp_*` scratchpads are accounted for in `LoreTabulaRasaReceipt.purgedCounts` and that `32.status.unit` (`StatusModel`) immediately reflects the post-purge Void State (`isVoidState: true`, `activeBorderColor: 'amber'`, `dispatchLockEngaged: true`) with the 4-line `GENESIS_MERKLE_ROOT_64` HUD banner.
- **The Technical Challenge:** Enforcing `assertSafeResetTarget` across every errata file and recursive scratchpad match (`series/`, `compiled/`, `lore/`, `data/lockfile/`) so protected files (`.gitkeep`, `.env`, `data/directive/`) are never touched, accurately reporting purged file counts in `LoreTabulaRasaReceipt`, and chaining `ActSts.EVALUATE_VOID_STATE` (`voidStateScan`) inside `executeTabulaRasaLore` so `voidStateVerified` fails closed if any residual series directory survives on disk.

**Interface**

- **`000.goblin-lore` (`packages/299.lore/00.lore.unit`):** Exports `purgeErrataAndScratchpads` alongside `scanLoreBlastRadius` and `executeTabulaRasaLore` in `buz/tabulaRasa.buzz.ts` and `lore.buzzer.ts`, routing every target through `assertSafeResetTarget` in `resetGuard.buzz.ts`.
- **Status & Telemetry Unit (`packages/299.lore/32.status.unit`):** Updates `voidStateScan` in `buz/voidStateScan.buzz.ts` to populate `StatusModel.hudLines` and `merkleRootHex: GENESIS_MERKLE_ROOT_64` when `isVoidState === true`, invoked by `executeTabulaRasaLore` via `ActSts.EVALUATE_VOID_STATE`.
- **Phase 4 Triple-Lock Curses Gate & Phase 5 TUI Switchboard (`98.menu.unit`) & `000.camp-candor-cauldron` (`packages/001.cauldron`):** Consumes `purgedCounts.errataFiles`, `purgedCounts.scratchpadFiles`, and `voidStateVerified` from `LoreTabulaRasaReceipt` to certify completion of **Sequence Step 4/4** in the Master Fleet Tabula Rasa Reset.

**Interaction**

- **Blessed Terminal Interface (`apps/995.library` / `packages/299.lore/98.menu.unit`):** When an operator or autonomous Coding Agent executes a Tabula Rasa Void-State reset in the Blessed cockpit (`npm run dev`), `executeTabulaRasaLore` streams `>> [TABULA_RASA:ERRATA] Purged <N> errata ledger(s) and <M> scratchpad file(s) [OK]` and `>> [TABULA_RASA:HUD] Status unit synchronized -> VOID STATE (T0=$0.00) | MERKLE=00000000... | LOCK=ENGAGED [OK]` into the Right Console Pane (`cns00` at `{ x: 4, y: 0, xSpan: 8, ySpan: 12 }`), while `StatusModel` re-arms the amber Void-State lockout banner (`ACTIVE SERIES : [NONE (UNBOUND)]`).
- **Verification Gauntlet (`vitest` & workspace checks):** Operators and CI runners verify Tasks 3.3 and 3.4 compliance at Stage $T_0 = \$0.00$ by running `npx vitest run packages/299.lore/00.lore.unit/test/tabulaRasaErrataStatusSync.test.ts`, `npm run check:types --workspace=@camp_candor/299.lore`, `npm run check:boundary --workspace=@camp_candor/299.lore`, and `npm run check:sentry --workspace=@camp_candor/299.lore`.

```bash
fs.mkdirSync(path.join(tmpRoot, 'series', 'gotham-trinity-crisis'), { recursive: true });
fs.mkdirSync(path.join(tmpRoot, 'compiled', 'gotham-trinity-crisis'), { recursive: true });

fs.mkdirSync(path.join(tmpRoot, 'data', 'lockfile'), { recursive: true });
fs.mkdirSync(path.join(tmpRoot, 'data', 'directive', 'day-001'), { recursive: true });

fs.writeFileSync(path.join(tmpRoot, 'series', '.gitkeep'), '');
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(tmpRoot, 'series', 'gotham-trinity-crisis', 'series.config.json'), '{}');
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(tmpRoot, 'series', 'gotham-trinity-crisis', '.tmp_draft.md'), 'temp');
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(tmpRoot, 'compiled', '.gitkeep'), '');
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(tmpRoot, 'compiled', 'gotham-trinity-crisis', 'bible-state.json'), '{}');
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(tmpRoot, 'lore', 'errata.json'), '{\"records\":[]}');
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(tmpRoot, 'lore', 'canon_errata_001.json'), '{}');
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(tmpRoot, 'lore', '.tmp_errata_stage.json'), '{}');
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(tmpRoot, 'data', 'lockfile', '.tmp_versions_conflict.json'), '{}');
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(tmpRoot, 'data', 'directive', 'day-001', 'protected.md'), 'keep');
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(tmpRoot, '.env'), 'SECRET=1');

const statusModel = new StatusModel();
statusModel.isVoidState = false;
statusModel.dispatchLockEngaged = false;
statusModel.activeBorderColor = 'green';
statusModel.discoveredSlugs = ['gotham-trinity-crisis'];

const mockState: any = {
  hunt: async (type: string, bale: any) => {
    if (type === '[Status action] Evaluate Void State') {
      await voidStateScan(statusModel, bale, mockState);
    }
    return {};
  }
};

let receipt: any = null;
await executeTabulaRasaLore(new LoreModel(), {
  idx: 'audit-33-34',
  src: tmpRoot,
  dat: {
    confirmToken1: TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_1,
    confirmToken2: TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_2,
    confirmToken3: TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_3
  },
  slv: (res: any) => { receipt = res.lorBit.dat; }
}, mockState);

if (!receipt || !receipt.ok || !receipt.voidStateVerified) {
  console.error(':: [FAIL] executeTabulaRasaLore failed voidStateVerified assertion:', receipt);
  process.exit(1);
}
if (receipt.purgedCounts.errataFiles !== 2 || receipt.purgedCounts.scratchpadFiles !== 3) {
  console.error(':: [FAIL] Errata/scratchpad purge counts inaccurate:', receipt.purgedCounts);
  process.exit(1);
}
if (fs.existsSync(path.join(tmpRoot, 'lore', 'errata.json')) || fs.existsSync(path.join(tmpRoot, 'data', 'lockfile', '.tmp_versions_conflict.json'))) {
  console.error(':: [FAIL] Errata ledger or scratchpad survived purge!');
  process.exit(1);
}
if (!fs.existsSync(path.join(tmpRoot, 'data', 'directive', 'day-001', 'protected.md')) || !fs.existsSync(path.join(tmpRoot, '.env'))) {
  console.error(':: [FAIL] Protected files were modified or deleted!');
  process.exit(1);
}
if (!statusModel.isVoidState || !statusModel.dispatchLockEngaged || statusModel.activeBorderColor !== 'amber' || !statusModel.hudLines.some(l => l.includes(GENESIS_MERKLE_ROOT_64))) {
  console.error(':: [FAIL] StatusModel failed to re-arm Void-State HUD:', statusModel);
  process.exit(1);
}

fs.rmSync(tmpRoot, { recursive: true, force: true });
console.log('>> [OK] Task 3.3 errata/scratchpad purge and Task 3.4 StatusModel Void-State sync verified.');
"
```

  - Once triple-confirmed, check whether `globalThis.CAULDRON` (or `globalThis.PIVOTS?.['@camp_candor/001.cauldron']` / `globalThis.PIVOTS?.['001.cauldron']`) is mounted on the shared bus:
    - **If Cauldron is mounted:** Dispatch `[Cauldron action] Master Fleet Tabula Rasa Reset` with `{ idx: 'lore-cauldron-cascade', dat: { confirmToken1, confirmToken2, confirmToken3, reverseCausalOrder: ['000.series-sower-conductor', '000.series-sower-engine', '000.goblin-spatial', '000.goblin-lore'] } }` and stream `>> [CAULDRON:CASCADE] Dispatched reverse-causal 4-repo reset to 000.camp-candor-cauldron [OK]` into `cns00`.

###### 1. `packages/299.lore/98.menu.unit/menu.action.ts` *(UPDATE)*

Preserve all existing actions (`INIT_MENU`, `UPDATE_MENU`, `OPEN_GENESIS_MENU`, `OPEN_COMPILE_MENU`, `OPEN_SCAFFOLD_MENU`, `OPEN_RETIREMENT_MENU`, `OPEN_HEALTH_MENU`, `OPEN_HYGIENE_MENU`, `OPEN_ERRATA_MENU`, `PUBLISH_SEALED_CANON`, `EXPORT_TOPOLOGY_MANIFEST`, `SELECT_DOMAIN_ENTITY_TYPE`, `OPEN_CHRONOLOGY_MENU`, `MANAGE_CHRONO_AGENDA`, `CONFIGURE_RECURRENCE_GRAMMAR`, `OPEN_AUTO_SCRIPT_MENU`, `CONFIGURE_PACING_BUDGET`, etc.) and add:

###### 2. `packages/299.lore/98.menu.unit/buz/tabulaRasaMenu.buzz.ts` *(CREATE / UPDATE)*

```typescript
/**
 * packages/299.lore/98.menu.unit/buz/tabulaRasaMenu.buzz.ts
 * Phase 4 & Phase 5 Blessed Terminal Interface Triple-Lock Tabula Rasa Reset
 * Switchboard and Cauldron Master Fleet Cascade Dispatcher for 299.lore.
 * Pure 7-bit ASCII compliance; 12x12 dual-pane grid discipline; zero console.log.
 */
import type State from '../../99.core/state.js'
import type { MenuModel } from '../menu.model.js'
import type MenuBit from '../fce/menu.bit.js'
import {
  TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_1,
  TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_2,
  TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_3,
  type LoreTabulaRasaReceipt,
  type LoreTabulaRasaRequestBit,
} from '../../38.types.unit/fce/tabulaRasa.interface.js'
import {
  executeTabulaRasaLore,
  scanLoreBlastRadius,
} from '../../00.lore.unit/buz/tabulaRasa.buzz.js'
import { resolveResetLoreRoot } from '../../00.lore.unit/buz/resetGuard.buzz.js'
import { LoreModel } from '../../00.lore.unit/lore.model.js'

const UPDATE_GRID = '[Grid action] Update Grid'
const OPEN_CHOICE = '[Open action] Open Choice'
const UPDATE_CONSOLE = '[Console action] Update Console'
const CAULDRON_MASTER_RESET_ACTION =
  '[Cauldron action] Master Fleet Tabula Rasa Reset'

export const TABULA_RASA_MENU_LABEL = '[Z] RESET LORE TO BYTE-0 VOID STATE'
export const CAULDRON_FLEET_RESET_LABEL =
  '[99] MASTER FLEET TABULA RASA RESET (CAULDRON CASCADE)'

const STAGE_1_NO = '[NO] CANCEL & RETURN (KEEP SYSTEM UNTOUCHED)'
const STAGE_1_YES = '[CONFIRM 1/3] INITIATE TABULA RASA VOID STATE RESET'

const STAGE_2_NO_A = '[NO] ABORT RESET IMMEDIATELY'
const STAGE_2_NO_B = '[NO] DO NOT WIPE GENERATED ASSETS'
const STAGE_2_YES =
  '[CONFIRM 2/3] ACKNOWLEDGE PERMANENT OBLITERATION OF ALL SERIES & LEDGERS'

const STAGE_3_NO = '[NO] STAND DOWN & DISARM RESET'
const STAGE_3_YES =
  '[CONFIRM 3/3] EXECUTE GRAND BEGINNING (RETURN TO BYTE-0 VOID STATE)'

function sanitizeAscii(raw: string): string {
  return String(raw || '')
    .replace(/[^\x20-\x7E]/g, '')
    .trim()
}

function yieldPaintCycle(): Promise<void> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => setImmediate(resolve))
}

async function emitConsoleLines(
  ste: State | undefined,
  lines: readonly string[],
): Promise<void> {
  const lib = (globalThis as any).LIBRARY || (global as any).LIBRARY
  for (const raw of lines) {
    const clean = sanitizeAscii(raw)
    if (lib && typeof lib.hunt === 'function') {
      await lib.hunt(UPDATE_CONSOLE, { idx: 'cns00', src: clean }).catch(() => {})
    } else if (ste && typeof ste.hunt === 'function') {
      await ste.hunt(UPDATE_CONSOLE, { idx: 'cns00', src: clean }).catch(() => {})
    }
  }
}

async function promptAlertChoiceModal(
  lst: readonly string[],
  tooltips: Record<string, string>,
  ste?: State,
): Promise<string> {
  const lib = (globalThis as any).LIBRARY || (global as any).LIBRARY
  if (!lib || typeof lib.hunt !== 'function') {
    return lst[0] || ''
  }

  const gridBit: any = await lib.hunt(UPDATE_GRID, {
    x: 0,
    y: 4,
    xSpan: 4,
    ySpan: 8,
  })

  const choiceBit: any = await lib.hunt(OPEN_CHOICE, {
    dat: {
      clr0: 'black',
      clr1: 'red',
      cb: (focusedLabel: string) => {
        const tip = tooltips[focusedLabel]
        if (tip) {
          emitConsoleLines(ste, [`>> [TIP] ${tip}`]).catch(() => {})
        }
      },
    },
    src: 'vertical',
    lst: [...lst],
    net: gridBit?.grdBit?.dat,
  })

  return String(choiceBit?.chcBit?.src || lst[0] || '')
}

function resolveCauldronPivot(): any {
  const g = globalThis as any
  if (g.CAULDRON && typeof g.CAULDRON.hunt === 'function') {
    return g.CAULDRON
  }
  const pivots = g.PIVOTS || {}
  return (
    pivots['@camp_candor/001.cauldron'] ||
    pivots['001.cauldron'] ||
    pivots['cauldron'] ||
    null
  )
}

function resetMenuModelToVoidState(
  cpy: MenuModel,
  receipt: LoreTabulaRasaReceipt,
): void {
  cpy.activeSelection = TABULA_RASA_MENU_LABEL
  cpy.lastActionTimestamp = Date.now()
  cpy.statusMessage = 'VOID_STATE'
  cpy.lastLifecycleReceipt = '[TABULA_RASA:OK]'
  cpy.lastSomaticReceipt = null
  cpy.activeSeries = undefined
  cpy.activeEntityDomain = undefined
  cpy.activeEntityPrefix = undefined
  cpy.activeEntityDirectory = undefined
  cpy.lastError = undefined
  cpy.lastTabulaRasaReceipt = receipt
  if (!Array.isArray(cpy.lastTelemetryReport)) {
    cpy.lastTelemetryReport = []
  }
  cpy.lastTelemetryReport.push(...receipt.receipts)
}

/**
 * Task 5.2: Dispatches the reverse-causal 4-repo Master Fleet Reset to
 * 000.camp-candor-cauldron if mounted on globalThis, or falls back cleanly
 * to local 299.lore Void-State reset when running standalone.
 */
export const triggerCauldronFleetResetCascade = async (
  cpy: MenuModel,
  bal?: MenuBit,
  ste?: State,
): Promise<MenuModel> => {
  const dat = (bal?.dat || {}) as Record<string, any>
  const req: LoreTabulaRasaRequestBit =
    (dat.tabulaRasaRequest as LoreTabulaRasaRequestBit) || dat
  const repoRoot = resolveResetLoreRoot(
    req.repoRoot || bal?.src || process.cwd(),
  )
  const lib = (globalThis as any).LIBRARY || (global as any).LIBRARY
  const isInteractive = Boolean(lib && typeof lib.hunt === 'function')

  let token1 = req.confirmToken1
  let token2 = req.confirmToken2
  let token3 = req.confirmToken3

  if (isInteractive && (!token1 || !token2 || !token3)) {
    await emitConsoleLines(ste, [
      '>> [CAULDRON:CASCADE] Initiating Triple-Lock Master Fleet Reset Gate (4 Repositories)...',
    ])

    const c1 = await promptAlertChoiceModal(
      [STAGE_1_NO, STAGE_1_YES],
      {
        [STAGE_1_NO]: 'Abort fleet reset and preserve all 4 repositories untouched.',
        [STAGE_1_YES]:
          'Arm Stage 1/3 for reverse-causal purge (conductor -> engine -> spatial -> lore).',
      },
      ste,
    )
    if (c1 !== STAGE_1_YES) {
      await emitConsoleLines(ste, [
        '>> [RESET:ABORTED] Cauldron cascade cancelled at Stage 1/3. System untouched.',
      ])
      if (bal?.slv) {
        bal.slv({
          mnuBit: { idx: 'cauldron-fleet-reset-aborted', val: 0 },
        })
      }
      return cpy
    }

    await emitConsoleLines(ste, [
      '>> [RESET:LOCK_1_CLEARED] Stage 1/3 verified. Awaiting Stage 2/3 confirmation...',
    ])
    await yieldPaintCycle()

    const c2 = await promptAlertChoiceModal(
      [STAGE_2_NO_A, STAGE_2_NO_B, STAGE_2_YES],
      {
        [STAGE_2_NO_A]: 'Abort fleet reset immediately.',
        [STAGE_2_NO_B]: 'Preserve generated screenplays, ledgers, and series.',
        [STAGE_2_YES]:
          'Acknowledge permanent deletion across conductor, engine, spatial, and lore.',
      },
      ste,
    )
    if (c2 !== STAGE_2_YES) {
      await emitConsoleLines(ste, [
        '>> [RESET:ABORTED] Cauldron cascade cancelled at Stage 2/3. System untouched.',
      ])
      if (bal?.slv) {
        bal.slv({
          mnuBit: { idx: 'cauldron-fleet-reset-aborted', val: 0 },
        })
      }
      return cpy
    }

    await emitConsoleLines(ste, [
      '>> [RESET:LOCK_2_CLEARED] Stage 2/3 verified. ARMED FOR FINAL FLEET EXECUTION (Stage 3/3)...',
    ])
    await yieldPaintCycle()

    const c3 = await promptAlertChoiceModal(
      [STAGE_3_NO, STAGE_3_YES],
      {
        [STAGE_3_NO]: 'Stand down and disarm Master Fleet Reset.',
        [STAGE_3_YES]:
          'Execute reverse-causal 4-repo wipe and return studio to Byte-0 Void State.',
      },
      ste,
    )
    if (c3 !== STAGE_3_YES) {
      await emitConsoleLines(ste, [
        '>> [RESET:ABORTED] Cauldron cascade cancelled at Stage 3/3. System untouched.',
      ])
      if (bal?.slv) {
        bal.slv({
          mnuBit: { idx: 'cauldron-fleet-reset-aborted', val: 0 },
        })
      }
      return cpy
    }

    token1 = TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_1
    token2 = TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_2
    token3 = TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_3
  }

  if (
    token1 !== TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_1 ||
    token2 !== TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_2 ||
    token3 !== TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_3
  ) {
    await emitConsoleLines(ste, [
      ':: [CAULDRON:ABORTED] Missing or invalid Triple-Lock confirmation tokens.',
    ])
    if (bal?.slv) {
      bal.slv({
        mnuBit: { idx: 'cauldron-fleet-reset-aborted', val: 0 },
      })
    }
    return cpy
  }

  const cauldronPivot = resolveCauldronPivot()
  if (cauldronPivot) {
    await emitConsoleLines(ste, [
      '>> [CAULDRON:CASCADE] Dispatching reverse-causal 4-repo reset to 000.camp-candor-cauldron...',
      '>> [CAULDRON:ORDER] 1:conductor -> 2:engine -> 3:spatial -> 4:lore',
    ])
    await yieldPaintCycle()

    let cauldronBit: any = null
    await cauldronPivot.hunt(CAULDRON_MASTER_RESET_ACTION, {
      idx: 'lore-cauldron-cascade',
      src: repoRoot,
      dat: {
        confirmToken1: token1,
        confirmToken2: token2,
        confirmToken3: token3,
        cascadeOrigin: 'CAULDRON_MASTER_CASCADE',
        reverseCausalOrder: [
          '000.series-sower-conductor',
          '000.series-sower-engine',
          '000.goblin-spatial',
          '000.goblin-lore',
        ],
      },
      slv: (res: any) => {
        cauldronBit = res?.cldBit || res
      },
    })

    await emitConsoleLines(ste, [
      '>> [CAULDRON:CASCADE] Reverse-causal 4-repo reset completed [OK]',
    ])

    if (bal?.slv) {
      bal.slv({
        mnuBit: {
          idx: 'cauldron-fleet-reset-pass',
          val: 1,
          dat: { mode: 'CAULDRON_CASCADE', cauldronBit },
        },
      })
    }
    return cpy
  }

  // Standalone fallback when 000.camp-candor-cauldron is not mounted on shared bus
  await emitConsoleLines(ste, [
    '>> [CAULDRON:WARN] 000.camp-candor-cauldron pivot not mounted on shared bus. Executing local 299.lore Void-State reset fallback...',
  ])
  await yieldPaintCycle()

  let localReceipt: LoreTabulaRasaReceipt | undefined
  await executeTabulaRasaLore(
    new LoreModel(),
    {
      idx: 'standalone-cauldron-fallback',
      src: repoRoot,
      dat: {
        repoRoot,
        confirmToken1: token1,
        confirmToken2: token2,
        confirmToken3: token3,
        cascadeOrigin: 'LOCAL_TUI',
      },
      slv: (res: any) => {
        localReceipt = res?.lorBit?.dat as LoreTabulaRasaReceipt
      },
    },
    ste,
  )

  if (localReceipt && localReceipt.ok) {
    resetMenuModelToVoidState(cpy, localReceipt)
  }

  if (bal?.slv) {
    bal.slv({
      mnuBit: {
        idx: localReceipt?.ok
          ? 'cauldron-fleet-reset-fallback-pass'
          : 'cauldron-fleet-reset-aborted',
        val: localReceipt?.ok ? 1 : 0,
        dat: { mode: 'STANDALONE_FALLBACK', receipt: localReceipt },
      },
    })
  }

  return cpy
}

/**
 * Tasks 5.1, 5.2 & 5.3: Interactive Blessed Terminal Triple-Lock Tabula Rasa Reset
 * Switchboard for 000.goblin-lore.
 */
export const runTabulaRasaMenu = async (
  cpy: MenuModel,
  bal?: MenuBit,
  ste?: State,
): Promise<MenuModel> => {
  const dat = (bal?.dat || {}) as Record<string, any>
  const req: LoreTabulaRasaRequestBit =
    (dat.tabulaRasaRequest as LoreTabulaRasaRequestBit) || dat
  const repoRoot = resolveResetLoreRoot(
    req.repoRoot || bal?.src || process.cwd(),
  )
  const lib = (globalThis as any).LIBRARY || (global as any).LIBRARY
  const isInteractive = Boolean(lib && typeof lib.hunt === 'function')

  // 1. Non-destructive pre-flight blast-radius scan
  const inventory = await scanLoreBlastRadius(repoRoot, ste)
  cpy.lastBlastRadiusInventory = inventory

  let token1 = req.confirmToken1
  let token2 = req.confirmToken2
  let token3 = req.confirmToken3

  if (isInteractive && (!token1 || !token2 || !token3)) {
    // Optional mode selection: Local 299.lore Reset vs. Master Cauldron Fleet Cascade
    const modeGrid: any = await lib.hunt(UPDATE_GRID, {
      x: 0,
      y: 4,
      xSpan: 4,
      ySpan: 8,
    })
    const modeChoice: any = await lib.hunt(OPEN_CHOICE, {
      dat: {
        clr0: 'black',
        clr1: 'yellow',
        cb: (focused: string) => {
          const desc =
            focused === CAULDRON_FLEET_RESET_LABEL
              ? 'Trigger reverse-causal 4-repo wipe via 000.camp-candor-cauldron.'
              : focused === TABULA_RASA_MENU_LABEL
                ? 'Execute Triple-Lock Void-State reset on local 000.goblin-lore.'
                : 'Return to Lore Flight Deck without modifying disk.'
          emitConsoleLines(ste, [`>> [TIP] ${desc}`]).catch(() => {})
        },
      },
      src: 'vertical',
      lst: [
        TABULA_RASA_MENU_LABEL,
        CAULDRON_FLEET_RESET_LABEL,
        '[<] CANCEL & RETURN',
      ],
      net: modeGrid?.grdBit?.dat,
    })

    const chosenMode = String(modeChoice?.chcBit?.src || '')
    if (chosenMode === CAULDRON_FLEET_RESET_LABEL) {
      return triggerCauldronFleetResetCascade(cpy, bal, ste)
    }
    if (chosenMode !== TABULA_RASA_MENU_LABEL) {
      await emitConsoleLines(ste, [
        '>> [RESET:ABORTED] Returned to Lore Menu without changes.',
      ])
      if (bal?.slv) {
        bal.slv({ mnuBit: { idx: 'tabula-rasa-menu-aborted', val: 0 } })
      }
      return cpy
    }

    await yieldPaintCycle()

    // Stage 1/3 Red Alert Confirmation Modal
    const c1 = await promptAlertChoiceModal(
      [STAGE_1_NO, STAGE_1_YES],
      {
        [STAGE_1_NO]: 'Cancel reset and preserve all series and compiled canon.',
        [STAGE_1_YES]: 'Confirm Stage 1/3 intent to wipe 000.goblin-lore.',
      },
      ste,
    )
    if (c1 !== STAGE_1_YES) {
      await emitConsoleLines(ste, [
        '>> [RESET:ABORTED] Triple-lock gate cancelled at Stage 1/3. System untouched.',
      ])
      if (bal?.slv) {
        bal.slv({ mnuBit: { idx: 'tabula-rasa-menu-aborted', val: 0 } })
      }
      return cpy
    }

    await emitConsoleLines(ste, [
      '>> [RESET:LOCK_1_CLEARED] Stage 1/3 verified. Awaiting Stage 2/3 confirmation...',
    ])
    await yieldPaintCycle()

    // Stage 2/3 Red Alert Confirmation Modal (shifted affirmative index)
    const c2 = await promptAlertChoiceModal(
      [STAGE_2_NO_A, STAGE_2_NO_B, STAGE_2_YES],
      {
        [STAGE_2_NO_A]: 'Abort reset immediately.',
        [STAGE_2_NO_B]: 'Do not wipe generated assets.',
        [STAGE_2_YES]:
          'Acknowledge permanent obliteration of series/, compiled/, and errata.',
      },
      ste,
    )
    if (c2 !== STAGE_2_YES) {
      await emitConsoleLines(ste, [
        '>> [RESET:ABORTED] Triple-lock gate cancelled at Stage 2/3. System untouched.',
      ])
      if (bal?.slv) {
        bal.slv({ mnuBit: { idx: 'tabula-rasa-menu-aborted', val: 0 } })
      }
      return cpy
    }

    await emitConsoleLines(ste, [
      '>> [RESET:LOCK_2_CLEARED] Stage 2/3 verified. ARMED FOR FINAL EXECUTION (Stage 3/3)...',
    ])
    await yieldPaintCycle()

    // Stage 3/3 Red Alert Confirmation Modal
    const c3 = await promptAlertChoiceModal(
      [STAGE_3_NO, STAGE_3_YES],
      {
        [STAGE_3_NO]: 'Stand down and disarm reset.',
        [STAGE_3_YES]:
          'Execute Grand Beginning and return 000.goblin-lore to Byte-0 Void State.',
      },
      ste,
    )
    if (c3 !== STAGE_3_YES) {
      await emitConsoleLines(ste, [
        '>> [RESET:ABORTED] Triple-lock gate cancelled at Stage 3/3. System untouched.',
      ])
      if (bal?.slv) {
        bal.slv({ mnuBit: { idx: 'tabula-rasa-menu-aborted', val: 0 } })
      }
      return cpy
    }

    token1 = TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_1
    token2 = TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_2
    token3 = TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_3
  }

  // 2. Execute atomic disk purge + in-memory unit resets + status HUD sync
  await yieldPaintCycle()
  let receipt: LoreTabulaRasaReceipt | undefined

  await executeTabulaRasaLore(
    new LoreModel(),
    {
      idx: 'menu-tabula-rasa-exec',
      src: repoRoot,
      dat: {
        repoRoot,
        confirmToken1: token1,
        confirmToken2: token2,
        confirmToken3: token3,
        cascadeOrigin: req.cascadeOrigin || 'LOCAL_TUI',
      },
      slv: (res: any) => {
        receipt = res?.lorBit?.dat as LoreTabulaRasaReceipt
      },
    },
    ste,
  )

  if (!receipt || !receipt.ok || receipt.aborted) {
    if (bal?.slv) {
      bal.slv({
        mnuBit: {
          idx: 'tabula-rasa-menu-aborted',
          val: 0,
          dat: receipt,
        },
      })
    }
    return cpy
  }

  // 3. Re-initialize MenuModel to Posture A Cold-Start Byte-0 Void State
  resetMenuModelToVoidState(cpy, receipt)

  const summaryReceipts = [
    `>> [TABULA_RASA:PURGE] Series=${receipt.purgedCounts.seriesDirectories} | Dossiers=${receipt.purgedCounts.markdownDossiers} | Compiled=${receipt.purgedCounts.compiledBibleManifests} | Errata=${receipt.purgedCounts.errataFiles} [OK]`,
    `>> [TABULA_RASA:MEMORY] Units reset (${receipt.unitsResetList.length}) + MenuModel unbound [OK]`,
    `>> [TABULA_RASA:HUD] Void State verified=${receipt.voidStateVerified} | Merkle=${receipt.merkleRootHex.slice(0, 8)}... [OK]`,
    '>> [TABULA_RASA:COMPLETE] 000.goblin-lore restored to Grand Beginning (Byte-0 Void State) [PASS]',
  ]

  await yieldPaintCycle()
  await emitConsoleLines(ste, summaryReceipts)

  if (bal?.slv) {
    bal.slv({
      mnuBit: {
        idx: 'tabula-rasa-menu-pass',
        val: 1,
        dat: receipt,
        lst: summaryReceipts,
      },
    })
  }

  return cpy
}
```

### **FUNCTIONAL MANDATE**

**User Story** It is 05:25 AM on the production floor of `000.goblin-lore`, and an autonomous Coding Agent has just completed a full screenplay production run for *Gotham Trinity Crisis* across the 12x12 Blessed Terminal Interface—generating character and grievance dossiers, sealing `bible-state.json`, exporting spatial proofs, and handing off the `.fountain` script and `dialogue_ast.json` to `000.series-sower-conductor`. Now the agent or human Showrunner needs a single, deterministic entry point right inside the primary Lore Flight Deck (`98.menu.unit`) to wipe either `000.goblin-lore` alone or trigger the reverse-causal four-repository Master Fleet Reset (`000.series-sower-conductor` $\rightarrow$ `000.series-sower-engine` $\rightarrow$ `000.goblin-spatial` $\rightarrow$ `000.goblin-lore`) via `000.camp-candor-cauldron`, returning the entire studio to **Posture A: Cold-Start Byte-0 Void State ($T_0 = \$0.00$)** before starting the next run. By implementing **Phase 5: Blessed Terminal Menu Switchboard Integration** in `packages/299.lore/98.menu.unit/`, `[Z] RESET LORE TO BYTE-0 VOID STATE` mounts directly on the Lore switchboard, routes into `runTabulaRasaMenu` and `triggerCauldronFleetResetCascade`, enforces the Triple-Lock red alert choice modals in the Left Control Pane (`{ x: 0, y: 4, xSpan: 4, ySpan: 8 }`), re-initializes `MenuModel` to `VOID_STATE`, and streams structured 7-bit ASCII receipts into `cns00` without tearing the 60 FPS curses buffer.

### II. BLESSED TERMINAL INTERFACE (TUI) VALIDATION & AUTONOMOUS REMEDIATION PROTOCOL

#### Blessed Terminal Interface (TUI) Validation Instructions

When validating features involving a Blessed Terminal Interface:

1. **Human Keyboard Emulation:** Manually interact with the interface using real keyboard inputs and sequences (e.g., Arrow keys, `Tab`, `Enter`, `Escape`, character inputs, and configured shortcuts) exactly as an operator at a physical terminal would.
2. **Step-by-Step Flow Navigation:** Drive end-to-end user journeys through sequential keystrokes rather than relying solely on headless mocks or direct state mutations. Allow render and event loop cycles between inputs.
3. **Screen & Focus Verification:** Observe terminal screen buffers, cursor positions, active focused widgets, and visual updates after each keypress sequence to ensure the UI responds correctly.
4. **Lifecycle & Clean Exit:** Verify proper startup rendering, navigation across interactive elements, and graceful termination routines (such as `q`, `Escape`, or `Ctrl+C`).

#### 1. Authoritative Runner Entry Point

- All interactive Blessed terminal tests **MUST** execute exclusively through:
  ```bash
  npm run dev
  ```
  (Note: passing extra package flags such as `npm run dev -- --package=299.lore` is forbidden; execute only bare `npm run dev`.)
- Direct execution via bare `node` is strictly prohibited because `.ts` transpilation requires `tsx`.

#### 2. Pre-Flight Boundary Self-Healing Guard

- Before launching the runner, the auditor must assert harness integrity:
  ```bash

import * as fs from 'node:fs';
import * as os from 'node:os';
import * as path from 'node:path';
import { MenuModel } from './packages/299.lore/98.menu.unit/menu.model.js';
import {

  triggerCauldronFleetResetCascade,
  TABULA_RASA_MENU_LABEL
} from './packages/299.lore/98.menu.unit/buz/tabulaRasaMenu.buzz.js';
import { LIFECYCLE_MENU_OPTIONS } from './packages/299.lore/98.menu.unit/buz/00.menu.buzz.js';
import {
  TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_1,
  TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_2,
  TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_3
} from './packages/299.lore/38.types.unit/fce/tabulaRasa.interface.js';

if (!LIFECYCLE_MENU_OPTIONS.includes(TABULA_RASA_MENU_LABEL)) {
  console.error(':: [FAIL] LIFECYCLE_MENU_OPTIONS missing TABULA_RASA_MENU_LABEL');
  process.exit(1);
}

const tmpRoot = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'lore-phase5-audit-'));
fs.mkdirSync(path.join(tmpRoot, 'packages', '299.lore'), { recursive: true });
fs.mkdirSync(path.join(tmpRoot, 'series', 'gotham-trinity-crisis'), { recursive: true });
fs.mkdirSync(path.join(tmpRoot, 'compiled', 'gotham-trinity-crisis'), { recursive: true });
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(tmpRoot, 'series', 'gotham-trinity-crisis', 'series.config.json'), '{}');
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(tmpRoot, 'compiled', 'gotham-trinity-crisis', 'bible-state.json'), '{}');

// 1. Verify mounted Cauldron cascade dispatch
let cauldronDispatched: any = null;
(globalThis as any).CAULDRON = {
  hunt: async (type: string, bale: any) => {
    cauldronDispatched = { type, bale };
    if (bale?.slv) bale.slv({ cldBit: { idx: 'master-tabula-rasa-pass', val: 1 } });
    return {};
  }
};

const model1 = new MenuModel();
await triggerCauldronFleetResetCascade(model1, {
  idx: 'audit-cauldron-mounted',
  src: tmpRoot,
  dat: {
    confirmToken1: TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_1,
    confirmToken2: TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_2,
    confirmToken3: TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_3
  }
});

if (!cauldronDispatched || cauldronDispatched.type !== '[Cauldron action] Master Fleet Tabula Rasa Reset') {
  console.error(':: [FAIL] triggerCauldronFleetResetCascade did not dispatch Master Fleet Tabula Rasa Reset to CAULDRON');
  process.exit(1);
}
delete (globalThis as any).CAULDRON;

// 2. Verify standalone fallback + MenuModel Void-State reset via runTabulaRasaMenu
const consoleLines: string[] = [];
const mockState: any = {
  hunt: async (type: string, bale: any) => {
    if (type === '[Console action] Update Console') {
      consoleLines.push(bale.src);
    }
    return {};
  }
};

const model2 = new MenuModel();
model2.activeSeries = 'gotham-trinity-crisis';
model2.activeEntityDomain = 'characters';
model2.statusMessage = 'ACTIVE';

let passBit: any = null;
await runTabulaRasaMenu(model2, {
  idx: 'audit-menu-reset',
  src: tmpRoot,
  dat: {
    confirmToken1: TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_1,
    confirmToken2: TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_2,
    confirmToken3: TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_3
  },
  slv: (res: any) => { passBit = res.mnuBit; }
}, mockState);

if (!passBit || passBit.idx !== 'tabula-rasa-menu-pass' || passBit.val !== 1) {
  console.error(':: [FAIL] runTabulaRasaMenu failed to resolve tabula-rasa-menu-pass');
  process.exit(1);
}
if (model2.activeSeries !== undefined || model2.activeEntityDomain !== undefined || model2.statusMessage !== 'VOID_STATE' || model2.lastLifecycleReceipt !== '[TABULA_RASA:OK]') {
  console.error(':: [FAIL] MenuModel fields were not reset to VOID_STATE:', model2);
  process.exit(1);
}
const requiredTags = ['[TABULA_RASA:PURGE]', '[TABULA_RASA:MEMORY]', '[TABULA_RASA:HUD]', '[TABULA_RASA:COMPLETE]'];
for (const tag of requiredTags) {
  if (!consoleLines.some(l => l.includes(tag))) {
    console.error(':: [FAIL] Missing cns00 telemetry receipt tag: ' + tag);
    process.exit(1);
  }
}

fs.rmSync(tmpRoot, { recursive: true, force: true });
console.log('>> [OK] Phase 5 switchboard routing, MenuModel reset, Cauldron cascade, and 7-bit telemetry verified.');
"
```
