###### 1. `packages/299.lore/43.compile.unit/buz/resetCoverageAudit.buzz.ts` *(CREATE)*

```typescript
/**
 * packages/299.lore/43.compile.unit/buz/resetCoverageAudit.buzz.ts
 * Phase 6 Static Unit Self-Verification Sentinel (Tasks 6.1 & 6.2).
 * Audits BEE.ts unit reset coverage against LORE_RESET_MANIFEST and certifies
 * round-trip Void-State parity at T_0 = $0.00 with zero standalone scripts.
 */
import * as fs from 'node:fs'
import * as path from 'node:path'
import type { CompileModel } from '../compile.model.js'
import type CompileBit from '../fce/compile.bit.js'
import type State from '../../99.core/state.js'
import {
  LORE_RESET_MANIFEST,
  LORE_UNIT_RESET_REGISTRY,
  validateLoreResetManifest,
} from '../../26.schema.unit/val/resetManifest.val.js'
import {
  GENESIS_MERKLE_ROOT_64,
  TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_1,
  TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_2,
  TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_3,
} from '../../38.types.unit/fce/tabulaRasa.interface.js'
import { executeTabulaRasaLore } from '../../00.lore.unit/buz/tabulaRasa.buzz.js'
import { LoreModel } from '../../00.lore.unit/lore.model.js'
import { TargetModel } from '../../34.target.unit/target.model.js'
import { resetTarget } from '../../34.target.unit/buz/target.buzz.js'
import { CanonModel } from '../../41.canon.unit/canon.model.js'
import { resetCanon } from '../../41.canon.unit/buz/canon.buzz.js'
import { CollectModel } from '../../97.collect.unit/collect.model.js'
import { resetCollect } from '../../97.collect.unit/buz/collect.buzz.js'
import { DatabaseModel } from '../../07.database.unit/database.model.js'
import { resetDatabase } from '../../07.database.unit/buz/database.buzz.js'
import { StatusModel } from '../../32.status.unit/status.model.js'
import {
  scanVoidState,
  voidStateScan,
} from '../../32.status.unit/buz/voidStateScan.buzz.js'

const UPDATE_CONSOLE = '[Console action] Update Console'

export interface LoreResetCoverageReport {
  readonly valid: boolean
  readonly packageRoot: string
  readonly mountedUnitCount: number
  readonly statefulUnitCount: number
  readonly statelessUnitCount: number
  readonly unhookedUnits: readonly string[]
  readonly missingWiringDiagnostics: readonly string[]
  readonly receipts: readonly string[]
}

export interface LoreVoidStateParityReport {
  readonly valid: boolean
  readonly sandboxRoot: string
  readonly initialVoidVerified: boolean
  readonly mutatedActiveVerified: boolean
  readonly postResetVoidVerified: boolean
  readonly zeroOrphanFilesVerified: boolean
  readonly zeroModelContaminationVerified: boolean
  readonly errors: readonly string[]
  readonly receipts: readonly string[]
}

function sanitizeAscii(raw: string): string {
  return String(raw || '')
    .replace(/[^\x20-\x7E]/g, '')
    .trim()
}

async function emitSentinelTelemetry(
  ste: State | undefined,
  lines: readonly string[],
): Promise<void> {
  const lib = (globalThis as any).LIBRARY || (global as any).LIBRARY
  for (const raw of lines) {
    const clean = sanitizeAscii(raw)
    if (lib && typeof lib.hunt === 'function') {
      await lib.hunt(UPDATE_CONSOLE, { idx: 'cns00', src: clean }).catch(() => {})
    } else if (ste && typeof ste.hunt === 'function') {
      await ste.hunt(UPDATE_CONSOLE, { idx: 'cns00', src: clean }).catch(() => {})
    }
  }
}

export function resolveLorePackageDirectory(
  startDir: string = process.cwd(),
): string {
  const resolvedStart = path.resolve(startDir)
  if (fs.existsSync(path.join(resolvedStart, 'BEE.ts'))) {
    return resolvedStart
  }
  const candidate = path.join(resolvedStart, 'packages', '299.lore')
  if (fs.existsSync(path.join(candidate, 'BEE.ts'))) {
    return candidate
  }
  let curr = resolvedStart
  while (curr && curr !== path.dirname(curr)) {
    const nested = path.join(curr, 'packages', '299.lore')
    if (fs.existsSync(path.join(nested, 'BEE.ts'))) {
      return nested
    }
    curr = path.dirname(curr)
  }
  return candidate
}

/**
 * Task 6.1: Pure BEE unit static self-verification sentinel auditing BEE.ts
 * unit registrations and stateful RESET_<UNIT> action/reducer wiring against
 * LORE_RESET_MANIFEST.
 */
export async function auditLoreResetCoverage(
  packageRootInput?: string,
  ste?: State,
): Promise<LoreResetCoverageReport> {
  const packageRoot = resolveLorePackageDirectory(
    packageRootInput || process.cwd(),
  )
  const beePath = path.join(packageRoot, 'BEE.ts')
  const unhookedUnits: string[] = []
  const missingWiringDiagnostics: string[] = []

  const manifestReport = validateLoreResetManifest(LORE_RESET_MANIFEST)
  if (!manifestReport.valid) {
    missingWiringDiagnostics.push(...manifestReport.errors)
  }

  let mountedUnits: string[] = []
  if (fs.existsSync(beePath)) {
    const beeSrc = fs.readFileSync(beePath, 'utf8')
    const matches = [...beeSrc.matchAll(/\.\/(\d{2}\.[a-z0-9-]+\.unit)\//g)].map(
      (m) => m[1],
    )
    mountedUnits = [...new Set(matches)].sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b))
  } else {
    missingWiringDiagnostics.push(`Missing BEE.ts manifest at ${beePath}`)
  }

  const registryMap = new Map(
    LORE_UNIT_RESET_REGISTRY.map((entry) => [entry.unitFolder, entry]),
  )

  for (const unitFolder of mountedUnits) {
    const descriptor = registryMap.get(unitFolder)
    if (!descriptor) {
      unhookedUnits.push(unitFolder)
      missingWiringDiagnostics.push(
        `Unregistered unit in BEE.ts: ${unitFolder} missing from LORE_UNIT_RESET_REGISTRY`,
      )
      continue
    }

    if (descriptor.classification === 'STATEFUL_RESETTABLE') {
      const unitSlug = unitFolder.replace(/^\d{2}\./, '').replace(/\.unit$/, '')
      const actionPath = path.join(
        packageRoot,
        unitFolder,
        `${unitSlug}.action.ts`,
      )
      const reducePath = path.join(
        packageRoot,
        unitFolder,
        `${unitSlug}.reduce.ts`,
      )
      const expectedConstant = `RESET_${unitSlug.toUpperCase().replace(/-/g, '_')}`

      if (fs.existsSync(actionPath)) {
        const actionSrc = fs.readFileSync(actionPath, 'utf8')
        if (
          !actionSrc.includes(expectedConstant) &&
          descriptor.resetActionConstant &&
          !actionSrc.includes(descriptor.resetActionConstant)
        ) {
          unhookedUnits.push(unitFolder)
          missingWiringDiagnostics.push(
            `Stateful unit ${unitFolder} missing ${expectedConstant} in ${unitSlug}.action.ts`,
          )
        }
      }

      if (fs.existsSync(reducePath)) {
        const reduceSrc = fs.readFileSync(reducePath, 'utf8')
        if (!reduceSrc.includes(`Act.${expectedConstant}`)) {
          unhookedUnits.push(unitFolder)
          missingWiringDiagnostics.push(
            `Stateful unit ${unitFolder} missing Act.${expectedConstant} case in ${unitSlug}.reduce.ts`,
          )
        }
      }
    }
  }

  const uniqueUnhooked = [...new Set(unhookedUnits)]
  const valid =
    mountedUnits.length > 0 &&
    uniqueUnhooked.length === 0 &&
    missingWiringDiagnostics.length === 0

  const receipts: string[] = [
    '>> ============================================================',
    '>> [SENTINEL:COVERAGE] TABULA RASA RESET COVERAGE AUDIT',
    `>> Package Root     : ${packageRoot}`,
    `>> Mounted Units    : ${mountedUnits.length} BEE.ts units scanned`,
    `>> Registry Split   : ${manifestReport.statefulUnitCount} stateful | ${manifestReport.statelessUnitCount} stateless`,
    `>> Unhooked Units   : ${uniqueUnhooked.length === 0 ? 'NONE [OK]' : uniqueUnhooked.join(', ')}`,
    `>> Sentinel Verdict : ${valid ? 'PASS [OK]' : 'FAIL [BREACH]'}`,
    '>> ============================================================',
  ]

  await emitSentinelTelemetry(ste, receipts)

  return {
    valid,
    packageRoot,
    mountedUnitCount: mountedUnits.length,
    statefulUnitCount: manifestReport.statefulUnitCount,
    statelessUnitCount: manifestReport.statelessUnitCount,
    unhookedUnits: uniqueUnhooked,
    missingWiringDiagnostics,
    receipts,
  }
}

/**
 * Task 6.2: Executes an isolated 4-step round-trip Void-State parity proof
 * (Void -> Mutated Gotham Crisis Corpus -> Triple-Lock Reset -> Void Parity).
 */
export async function verifyRoundTripVoidStateParity(
  sandboxRoot: string,
  ste?: State,
): Promise<LoreVoidStateParityReport> {
  const errors: string[] = []
  const resolvedRoot = path.resolve(sandboxRoot)

  // Step 1: Verify initial Void State
  const initialScan = scanVoidState(resolvedRoot)
  const initialVoidVerified =
    initialScan.isVoidState && initialScan.discoveredSlugs.length === 0
  if (!initialVoidVerified) {
    errors.push('Sandbox did not start in clean Void State')
  }

  // Step 2: Populate synthetic Gotham series, compiled artifacts, errata, SQLite, and models
  const seriesDir = path.join(resolvedRoot, 'series', 'gotham-trinity-crisis')
  const compiledDir = path.join(
    resolvedRoot,
    'compiled',
    'gotham-trinity-crisis',
  )
  const loreDir = path.join(resolvedRoot, 'lore')
  const sqliteDir = path.join(resolvedRoot, 'data', 'sqlite')

  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(seriesDir, 'characters'), { recursive: true })
  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(seriesDir, 'locations'), { recursive: true })
  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(seriesDir, 'possessions'), { recursive: true })
  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(seriesDir, 'grievances'), { recursive: true })
  fs.mkdirSync(compiledDir, { recursive: true })
  fs.mkdirSync(loreDir, { recursive: true })
  fs.mkdirSync(sqliteDir, { recursive: true })

  fs.writeFileSync(
    path.join(seriesDir, 'series.config.json'),
    JSON.stringify({ series_slug: 'gotham-trinity-crisis' }),
    'utf8',
  )
  fs.writeFileSync(
    path.join(seriesDir, 'characters', 'char_wonder_woman.md'),
    '---\nid: char_wonder_woman\n---\n',
    'utf8',
  )
  fs.writeFileSync(
    path.join(seriesDir, 'characters', 'char_batman.md'),
    '---\nid: char_batman\n---\n',
    'utf8',
  )
  fs.writeFileSync(
    path.join(seriesDir, 'characters', 'char_joker.md'),
    '---\nid: char_joker\n---\n',
    'utf8',
  )
  fs.writeFileSync(
    path.join(compiledDir, 'bible-state.json'),
    JSON.stringify({ seriesSlug: 'gotham-trinity-crisis' }),
    'utf8',
  )
  fs.writeFileSync(
    path.join(compiledDir, 'topology.manifest.json'),
    JSON.stringify({ locationId: 'loc_gotham_rooftop' }),
    'utf8',
  )
  fs.writeFileSync(
    path.join(loreDir, 'errata.json'),
    JSON.stringify({ records: [{ id: 'err_01' }] }),
    'utf8',
  )
  fs.writeFileSync(
    path.join(sqliteDir, 'gotham-trinity-crisis.local.db'),
    'sqlite-bytes',
    'utf8',
  )

  const mutatedScan = scanVoidState(resolvedRoot)
  const mutatedActiveVerified =
    !mutatedScan.isVoidState &&
    mutatedScan.discoveredSlugs.includes('gotham-trinity-crisis')
  if (!mutatedActiveVerified) {
    errors.push('Synthetic series mutation failed to activate corpus state')
  }

  // Dirty in-memory models
  const loreModel = new LoreModel()
  loreModel.loreBitList = [{ idx: 'dirty' }]
  const targetModel = new TargetModel()
  targetModel.activeSeriesSlug = 'gotham-trinity-crisis'
  const canonModel = new CanonModel()
  canonModel.factStandings.set('f1', { factId: 'f1' } as any)
  const collectModel = new CollectModel()
  collectModel.caboodleBits = { characters: 0 }
  const dbModel = new DatabaseModel()
  dbModel.activeDbPath = path.join(sqliteDir, 'gotham-trinity-crisis.local.db')
  const statusModel = new StatusModel()
  statusModel.isVoidState = false
  statusModel.dispatchLockEngaged = false
  statusModel.activeBorderColor = 'green'

  const localBus: any = {
    hunt: async (type: string, bale: any) => {
      switch (type) {
        case '[Target action] Reset Target':
          await resetTarget(targetModel, bale, localBus)
          break
        case '[Canon action] Reset Canon':
          await resetCanon(canonModel, bale, localBus)
          break
        case '[Collect action] Reset Collect':
          await resetCollect(collectModel, bale, localBus)
          break
        case '[Database action] Reset Database':
          await resetDatabase(dbModel, bale, localBus)
          break
        case '[Status action] Evaluate Void State':
          await voidStateScan(statusModel, bale, localBus)
          break
      }
      return {}
    },
  }

  // Step 3: Execute Triple-Lock Tabula Rasa Reset
  await executeTabulaRasaLore(
    loreModel,
    {
      idx: 'round-trip-parity-reset',
      src: resolvedRoot,
      dat: {
        repoRoot: resolvedRoot,
        confirmToken1: TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_1,
        confirmToken2: TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_2,
        confirmToken3: TABULA_RASA_TOKEN_3,
      },
    },
    localBus,
  )

  // Step 4: Assert bit-for-bit Void-State parity
  const postScan = scanVoidState(resolvedRoot)
  const postResetVoidVerified =
    postScan.isVoidState && postScan.discoveredSlugs.length === 0
  if (!postResetVoidVerified) {
    errors.push('Post-reset filesystem scan did not return to Void State')
  }

  const zeroOrphanFilesVerified =
    !fs.existsSync(seriesDir) &&
    !fs.existsSync(compiledDir) &&
    !fs.existsSync(path.join(loreDir, 'errata.json')) &&
    !fs.existsSync(path.join(sqliteDir, 'gotham-trinity-crisis.local.db'))
  if (!zeroOrphanFilesVerified) {
    errors.push('Orphan files survived Triple-Lock Tabula Rasa Reset')
  }

  const zeroModelContaminationVerified =
    loreModel.loreBitList.length === 0 &&
    targetModel.activeSeriesSlug === null &&
    canonModel.factStandings.size === 0 &&
    Object.keys(collectModel.caboodleBits).length === 0 &&
    dbModel.activeDbPath === null &&
    statusModel.isVoidState === true &&
    statusModel.dispatchLockEngaged === true &&
    statusModel.activeBorderColor === 'amber' &&
    statusModel.hudLines.some((l) => l.includes(GENESIS_MERKLE_ROOT_64))
  if (!zeroModelContaminationVerified) {
    errors.push('In-memory model contamination survived Tabula Rasa Reset')
  }

  const valid = errors.length === 0
  const receipts: string[] = [
    `>> [SENTINEL:PARITY] Round-trip Void-State parity certified [${valid ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'}]`,
  ]
  await emitSentinelTelemetry(ste, receipts)

  return {
    valid,
    sandboxRoot: resolvedRoot,
    initialVoidVerified,
    mutatedActiveVerified,
    postResetVoidVerified,
    zeroOrphanFilesVerified,
    zeroModelContaminationVerified,
    errors,
    receipts,
  }
}

export const auditLoreResetCoverageAction = async (
  cpy: CompileModel,
  bal?: CompileBit,
  ste?: State,
): Promise<CompileModel> => {
  const report = await auditLoreResetCoverage(bal?.src, ste)
  if (bal?.slv) {
    bal.slv({
      cmpBit: {
        idx: report.valid
          ? 'audit-lore-reset-coverage-pass'
          : 'audit-lore-reset-coverage-fail',
        val: report.valid ? 0 : -1,
        dat: report,
      },
    })
  }
  return cpy
}

export const auditVoidStateParityAction = async (
  cpy: CompileModel,
  bal?: CompileBit,
  ste?: State,
): Promise<CompileModel> => {
  const targetRoot = bal?.src || process.cwd()
  const report = await verifyRoundTripVoidStateParity(targetRoot, ste)
  if (bal?.slv) {
    bal.slv({
      cmpBit: {
        idx: report.valid
          ? 'audit-void-state-parity-pass'
          : 'audit-void-state-parity-fail',
        val: report.valid ? 0 : -1,
        dat: report,
      },
    })
  }
  return cpy
}
```

### II. BLESSED TERMINAL INTERFACE (TUI) VALIDATION & AUTONOMOUS REMEDIATION PROTOCOL

#### Blessed Terminal Interface (TUI) Validation Instructions

When validating features involving a Blessed Terminal Interface:

1. **Human Keyboard Emulation:** Manually interact with the interface using real keyboard inputs and sequences (e.g., Arrow keys, `Tab`, `Enter`, `Escape`, character inputs, and configured shortcuts) exactly as an operator at a physical terminal would.
2. **Step-by-Step Flow Navigation:** Drive end-to-end user journeys through sequential keystrokes rather than relying solely on headless mocks or direct state mutations. Allow render and event loop cycles between inputs.
3. **Screen & Focus Verification:** Observe terminal screen buffers, cursor positions, active focused widgets, and visual updates after each keypress sequence to ensure the UI responds correctly.
4. **Lifecycle & Clean Exit:** Verify proper startup rendering, navigation across interactive elements, and graceful termination routines (such as `q`, `Escape`, or `Ctrl+C`).

#### 1. Authoritative Runner Entry Point

- All interactive Blessed terminal tests **MUST** execute exclusively through:
  ```bash
  npm run dev
  ```
  (Note: passing extra package flags such as `npm run dev -- --package=299.lore` is forbidden; execute only bare `npm run dev`.)
- Direct execution via bare `node` is strictly prohibited because `.ts` transpilation requires `tsx`.

import * as fs from 'node:fs';
import * as os from 'node:os';
import * as path from 'node:path';
import {
  auditLoreResetCoverage,
  verifyRoundTripVoidStateParity
} from './packages/299.lore/43.compile.unit/buz/resetCoverageAudit.buzz.js';

// 1. Live package coverage check
const liveReport = await auditLoreResetCoverage('packages/299.lore');
if (!liveReport.valid || liveReport.unhookedUnits.length !== 0 || liveReport.mountedUnitCount < 47) {
  console.error(':: [FAIL] Live packages/299.lore failed reset coverage audit:', liveReport);
  process.exit(1);
}

// 2. Negative-control trap: unhooked 95.rogue.unit in BEE.ts
const tmpPkg = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'lore-phase6-rogue-'));
const beeOrig = fs.readFileSync('packages/299.lore/BEE.ts', 'utf8');
fs.writeFileSync(
  path.join(tmpPkg, 'BEE.ts'),
  beeOrig + \"\nimport RogueUnit from './95.rogue.unit/rogue.unit'\n\",

  process.exit(1);
}

// 3. Round-trip Void-State parity check in isolated sandbox
const tmpSandbox = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'lore-phase6-parity-'));
const parityReport = await verifyRoundTripVoidStateParity(tmpSandbox);
if (!parityReport.valid || parityReport.errors.length !== 0) {
  console.error(':: [FAIL] Round-trip Void-State parity check failed:', parityReport);
  process.exit(1);
}
fs.rmSync(tmpSandbox, { recursive: true, force: true });

console.log('>> [OK] Live 47-unit coverage, rogue unit trap, and 4-step round-trip Void-State parity certified.');
"
